            "STAGE 5 interface ready: decision_engine.py translates MC results into transparent cost/risk decision indicators.",
            "STAGE 5 safety: decision grades never feed back into NSGA and no automatic best alternative is selected.",
            "STAGE 6 interface ready: audit_engine.py performs read-only cross-stage consistency and regression checks.",
            "STAGE 6 safety: audit results never modify NSGA, Monte Carlo, representative strategies, or decision grades.",
        ],
    }
    return result

# ============================================================================
# FULL PIPELINE STANDALONE CLI
# NSGA -> Monte Carlo -> Cost/Risk Decision -> Audit -> Dashboard Workbook

import types as _types
import json as _json
import time as _time
import traceback as _traceback
from pathlib import Path as _Path

_SIMULATION_ENGINE_SOURCE = '# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-\n"""\nsimulation_engine.py\n====================\n\nFINAL DEBUGGED · Cold-start Monte Carlo engine\n\nCanonical logic restored\n------------------------\n1. K is generated PER PART × PERIOD × ITERATION.\n2. The same part-level K and same demand event times are shared across all\n   representative strategies (Common Random Numbers).\n3. Demand count:\n      D_i ~ Poisson(Lambda_Annual_MC_Base_i × K_i × years)\n4. Event times are uniform over the analysis horizon conditional on demand count.\n5. If stock is available:\n      immediate fulfillment, stock -1, replenishment arrival scheduled after\n      Repair_TAT_H (repairable) or Lead_Time_H (non-repairable).\n6. If stock is unavailable:\n      shortage event; delay equals the engineering replenishment delay.\n      The backorder is satisfied by that delivery itself, so no extra inventory\n      increment is created.\n7. Three service aggregation methods are returned:\n      - 월 동일 가중\n      - 건수 가중\n      - 가중평균 (50% 월 동일 가중 + 50% 건수 가중)\n8. Decision Layer should use "건수 가중" as the primary physical comparison.\n\nNo historical demand is used to tune the alternatives.\n"""\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport heapq\nimport math\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom typing import Callable, Dict, List, Optional\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\n\n\nZ_95 = 1.6448536269514722\nDAYS_PER_YEAR = 365.25\nHOURS_PER_DAY = 24.0\nWEIGHTED_BLEND_ALPHA = 0.50\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass MonteCarloConfig:\n    iterations: int = 3000\n    random_seed: int = 42\n    period_months: List[int] = field(default_factory=lambda: [12, 24, 36])\n\n    k_distribution: str = "lognormal"\n    k_median: float = 1.0\n    k_p05: float = 0.5\n    k_p95: float = 2.0\n\n    common_random_numbers: bool = True\n    min_cycle_time_h: float = 1.0\n    store_iteration_detail: bool = True\n    weighted_blend_alpha: float = WEIGHTED_BLEND_ALPHA\n\n\ndef validate_monte_carlo_config(config: MonteCarloConfig) -> Dict[str, object]:\n    issues: List[str] = []\n    if int(config.iterations) <= 0:\n        issues.append("iterations must be > 0.")\n    periods = [int(x) for x in config.period_months]\n    if not periods or any(x <= 0 for x in periods):\n        issues.append("period_months must contain positive month values.")\n    if str(config.k_distribution).strip().lower() != "lognormal":\n        issues.append("Only lognormal K is supported.")\n    if not (0 < float(config.k_p05) <= float(config.k_median) <= float(config.k_p95)):\n        issues.append("K anchors must satisfy 0 < P05 <= median <= P95.")\n    if float(config.min_cycle_time_h) <= 0:\n        issues.append("min_cycle_time_h must be > 0.")\n    if not (0.0 <= float(config.weighted_blend_alpha) <= 1.0):\n        issues.append("weighted_blend_alpha must be between 0 and 1.")\n    return {"ready": len(issues) == 0, "issues": issues}\n\n\ndef _lognormal_parameters(config: MonteCarloConfig) -> tuple[float, float]:\n    med = max(float(config.k_median), 1e-12)\n    p05 = max(float(config.k_p05), 1e-12)\n    p95 = max(float(config.k_p95), p05)\n    mu = math.log(med)\n    sigma_low = abs(math.log(med / p05)) / Z_95 if p05 < med else 0.0\n    sigma_high = abs(math.log(p95 / med)) / Z_95 if p95 > med else 0.0\n    vals = [x for x in (sigma_low, sigma_high) if x > 0]\n    sigma = float(np.mean(vals)) if vals else 1e-9\n    return mu, max(sigma, 1e-9)\n\n\ndef _validate_bridge_df(df: pd.DataFrame) -> None:\n    required = [\n        "Alternative_ID", "Strategy", "Part_ID", "Stock_Qty",\n        "Lambda_Annual_MC_Base", "Lead_Time_H",\n        "Repairable_Flag", "Repair_TAT_H",\n    ]\n    missing = [c for c in required if c not in df.columns]\n    if missing:\n        raise ValueError(f"monte_carlo_bridge_df missing required columns: {missing}")\n    if df.empty:\n        raise ValueError("monte_carlo_bridge_df is empty.")\n\n\ndef _prepare_bridge(df: pd.DataFrame) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, list[str], list[str]]:\n    _validate_bridge_df(df)\n    out = df.copy()\n    out["Alternative_ID"] = out["Alternative_ID"].astype(str)\n    out["Strategy"] = out["Strategy"].astype(str)\n    out["Part_ID"] = out["Part_ID"].astype(str)\n\n    for c, default in {\n        "Stock_Qty": 0,\n        "Lambda_Annual_MC_Base": 0.0,\n        "Lead_Time_H": 0.0,\n        "Repairable_Flag": 0,\n        "Repair_TAT_H": 0.0,\n    }.items():\n        out[c] = pd.to_numeric(out[c], errors="coerce").fillna(default)\n\n    out["Stock_Qty"] = out["Stock_Qty"].clip(lower=0).astype(int)\n    out["Lambda_Annual_MC_Base"] = out["Lambda_Annual_MC_Base"].clip(lower=0.0)\n    out["Lead_Time_H"] = out["Lead_Time_H"].clip(lower=0.0)\n    out["Repairable_Flag"] = out["Repairable_Flag"].clip(lower=0, upper=1).astype(int)\n    out["Repair_TAT_H"] = out["Repair_TAT_H"].clip(lower=0.0)\n\n    # Physical parameters must be invariant across representative alternatives.\n    for pid, sub in out.groupby("Part_ID", sort=False):\n        for col in ["Lambda_Annual_MC_Base", "Lead_Time_H", "Repairable_Flag", "Repair_TAT_H"]:\n            if pd.to_numeric(sub[col], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).nunique(dropna=False) > 1:\n                raise ValueError(\n                    f"Bridge physical parameter differs across alternatives: Part_ID={pid}, column={col}"\n                )\n\n    return (\n        out,\n        out["Alternative_ID"].drop_duplicates().tolist(),\n        sorted(out["Part_ID"].drop_duplicates().tolist()),\n    )\n\n\ndef _simulate_part(\n    event_times_days: np.ndarray,\n    initial_stock: int,\n    replenishment_delay_days: float,\n) -> tuple[int, int, float, list[float]]:\n    """\n    Canonical Stage-4 part event logic.\n\n    Returns:\n      immediate_count, shortage_count, total_shortage_delay_days, delays_per_demand\n    """\n    on_hand = max(int(initial_stock), 0)\n    delay = max(float(replenishment_delay_days), 0.0)\n    arrivals: List[float] = []\n\n    immediate = 0\n    shortage = 0\n    shortage_delay = 0.0\n    delays: List[float] = []\n\n    for t in event_times_days:\n        t = float(t)\n        while arrivals and arrivals[0] <= t:\n            heapq.heappop(arrivals)\n            on_hand += 1\n\n        if on_hand > 0:\n            on_hand -= 1\n            immediate += 1\n            delays.append(0.0)\n            heapq.heappush(arrivals, t + delay)\n        else:\n            shortage += 1\n            shortage_delay += delay\n            delays.append(delay)\n            # Backorder is satisfied by its own delivery; no inventory increment.\n\n    return immediate, shortage, shortage_delay, delays\n\n\ndef _pct(values, q):\n    a = np.asarray(values, dtype=float)\n    return float(np.quantile(a, q)) if a.size else 0.0\n\n\ndef run_coldstart_monte_carlo(\n    bridge_df: pd.DataFrame,\n    config: Optional[MonteCarloConfig] = None,\n    progress_callback: Optional[Callable[[Dict[str, object]], None]] = None,\n) -> Dict[str, object]:\n    if config is None:\n        config = MonteCarloConfig()\n\n    check = validate_monte_carlo_config(config)\n    if not check["ready"]:\n        raise ValueError("Invalid MonteCarloConfig: " + "; ".join(check["issues"]))\n\n    bridge, alt_ids, part_ids = _prepare_bridge(bridge_df)\n    periods = [int(x) for x in config.period_months]\n    n_iter = int(config.iterations)\n\n    alt_meta = (\n        bridge[["Alternative_ID", "Strategy"]]\n        .drop_duplicates("Alternative_ID")\n        .set_index("Alternative_ID")\n    )\n\n    physical = (\n        bridge.sort_values(["Part_ID", "Alternative_ID"])\n        .drop_duplicates("Part_ID")\n        .set_index("Part_ID")\n        .reindex(part_ids)\n    )\n    lam_y = pd.to_numeric(physical["Lambda_Annual_MC_Base"], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).to_numpy(float)\n    lead_h = pd.to_numeric(physical["Lead_Time_H"], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).to_numpy(float)\n    repairable = pd.to_numeric(physical["Repairable_Flag"], errors="coerce").fillna(0).astype(int).to_numpy()\n    tat_h = pd.to_numeric(physical["Repair_TAT_H"], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).to_numpy(float)\n\n    cycle_h = np.where((repairable > 0) & (tat_h > 0), tat_h, lead_h)\n    cycle_h = np.maximum(cycle_h, float(config.min_cycle_time_h))\n    cycle_days = cycle_h / HOURS_PER_DAY\n\n    part_pos = {p: i for i, p in enumerate(part_ids)}\n    alt_pos = {a: i for i, a in enumerate(alt_ids)}\n    stock = np.zeros((len(alt_ids), len(part_ids)), dtype=int)\n    for _, r in bridge[["Alternative_ID", "Part_ID", "Stock_Qty"]].iterrows():\n        stock[alt_pos[str(r["Alternative_ID"])], part_pos[str(r["Part_ID"])]] = int(r["Stock_Qty"])\n\n    mu_k, sigma_k = _lognormal_parameters(config)\n\n    iteration_rows: List[Dict[str, object]] = []\n    part_agg: Dict[tuple[int, int], Dict[str, np.ndarray]] = {}\n    total_work = len(periods) * n_iter\n    done = 0\n\n    for months in periods:\n        years = float(months) / 12.0\n        horizon_days = years * DAYS_PER_YEAR\n        month_days = horizon_days / float(months)\n\n        for ai in range(len(alt_ids)):\n            key = (months, ai)\n            part_agg[key] = {\n                "demand": np.zeros(len(part_ids), float),\n                "shortage": np.zeros(len(part_ids), float),\n                "immediate": np.zeros(len(part_ids), float),\n                "delay": np.zeros(len(part_ids), float),\n            }\n\n        # Period-specific RNG. Part-level draws are generated once and shared across alternatives.\n        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(config.random_seed + months * 100003))\n\n        for it in range(1, n_iter + 1):\n            k_by_part = rng.lognormal(mean=mu_k, sigma=sigma_k, size=len(part_ids))\n            events_by_part: List[np.ndarray] = []\n            demand_counts = np.zeros(len(part_ids), dtype=int)\n\n            for pi, base_lam in enumerate(lam_y):\n                mu = max(float(base_lam) * float(k_by_part[pi]) * years, 0.0)\n                n = int(rng.poisson(mu))\n                demand_counts[pi] = n\n                if n > 0:\n                    ev = np.sort(rng.uniform(0.0, horizon_days, size=n)).astype(float)\n                else:\n                    ev = np.empty(0, float)\n                events_by_part.append(ev)\n\n            total_demand = int(demand_counts.sum())\n\n            for ai, aid in enumerate(alt_ids):\n                immediate_total = 0\n                shortage_total = 0\n                shortage_delay_total = 0.0\n                all_delays: List[float] = []\n                monthly_demand = np.zeros(months, dtype=int)\n                monthly_immediate = np.zeros(months, dtype=int)\n\n                for pi, events in enumerate(events_by_part):\n                    if demand_counts[pi] <= 0:\n                        continue\n\n                    imm, sh, sh_delay, delays = _simulate_part(\n                        events,\n                        int(stock[ai, pi]),\n                        float(cycle_days[pi]),\n                    )\n                    immediate_total += imm\n                    shortage_total += sh\n                    shortage_delay_total += sh_delay\n                    all_delays.extend(delays)\n\n                    for t, dly in zip(events, delays):\n                        mi = min(months - 1, int(float(t) / max(month_days, 1e-12)))\n                        monthly_demand[mi] += 1\n                        if dly <= 1e-12:\n                            monthly_immediate[mi] += 1\n\n                    key = (months, ai)\n                    part_agg[key]["demand"][pi] += int(demand_counts[pi])\n                    part_agg[key]["shortage"][pi] += sh\n                    part_agg[key]["immediate"][pi] += imm\n                    part_agg[key]["delay"][pi] += sh_delay\n\n                case_weighted = 1.0 if total_demand <= 0 else float(immediate_total / total_demand)\n                active = monthly_demand > 0\n                if active.any():\n                    monthly_equal = float(np.mean(monthly_immediate[active] / monthly_demand[active]))\n                else:\n                    monthly_equal = 1.0\n                blend = (\n                    float(config.weighted_blend_alpha) * monthly_equal\n                    + (1.0 - float(config.weighted_blend_alpha)) * case_weighted\n                )\n\n                mean_event_delay = float(np.mean(all_delays)) if all_delays else 0.0\n                p95_event_delay = float(np.percentile(all_delays, 95)) if all_delays else 0.0\n\n                if config.store_iteration_detail:\n                    iteration_rows.append({\n                        "Alternative_ID": aid,\n                        "Strategy": str(alt_meta.loc[aid, "Strategy"]),\n                        "Period_Months": months,\n                        "Iteration": it,\n                        "Mean_K": float(np.mean(k_by_part)) if len(k_by_part) else 1.0,\n                        "P95_K": float(np.percentile(k_by_part, 95)) if len(k_by_part) else 1.0,\n                        "Demand_Total": total_demand,\n                        "Immediate_Fulfilled": int(immediate_total),\n                        "Shortage_Count": int(shortage_total),\n                        "Case_Weighted_Service": case_weighted,\n                        "Monthly_Equal_Service": monthly_equal,\n                        "Bridge_Weighted_Service": blend,\n                        "Zero_Shortage": int(shortage_total == 0),\n                        "Mean_Event_Delay_Days": mean_event_delay,\n                        "P95_Event_Delay_Days": p95_event_delay,\n                        "Total_Shortage_Delay_Days": float(shortage_delay_total),\n                    })\n\n            done += 1\n            if progress_callback is not None and (\n                it == 1 or it == n_iter or it % max(1, n_iter // 30) == 0\n            ):\n                progress_callback({\n                    "Period_Months": months,\n                    "Iteration": it,\n                    "Iterations": n_iter,\n                    "Progress": float(done / max(total_work, 1)),\n                })\n\n    iteration_df = pd.DataFrame(iteration_rows)\n\n    method_map = {\n        "월 동일 가중": "Monthly_Equal_Service",\n        "건수 가중": "Case_Weighted_Service",\n        "가중평균": "Bridge_Weighted_Service",\n    }\n    summary_rows: List[Dict[str, object]] = []\n    for (aid, strategy, months), sub in iteration_df.groupby(\n        ["Alternative_ID", "Strategy", "Period_Months"], sort=False\n    ):\n        for method, score_col in method_map.items():\n            scores = pd.to_numeric(sub[score_col], errors="coerce").fillna(1.0).to_numpy(float)\n            demand = pd.to_numeric(sub["Demand_Total"], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).to_numpy(float)\n            shortage = pd.to_numeric(sub["Shortage_Count"], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).to_numpy(float)\n            zero = pd.to_numeric(sub["Zero_Shortage"], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).to_numpy(float)\n            mean_delay = pd.to_numeric(sub["Mean_Event_Delay_Days"], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).to_numpy(float)\n            p95_delay = pd.to_numeric(sub["P95_Event_Delay_Days"], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).to_numpy(float)\n\n            summary_rows.append({\n                "Alternative_ID": aid,\n                "Strategy": strategy,\n                "Period_Months": int(months),\n                "Evaluation_Method": method,\n                "Iterations": int(len(sub)),\n                "Service_Mean": float(np.mean(scores)),\n                "Service_P05": _pct(scores, 0.05),\n                "Service_P50": _pct(scores, 0.50),\n                "Service_P95": _pct(scores, 0.95),\n                "Probability_Zero_Shortage": float(np.mean(zero)),\n                "Demand_Mean": float(np.mean(demand)),\n                "Demand_P05": _pct(demand, 0.05),\n                "Demand_P50": _pct(demand, 0.50),\n                "Demand_P95": _pct(demand, 0.95),\n                "Shortage_Mean": float(np.mean(shortage)),\n                "Shortage_P95": _pct(shortage, 0.95),\n                "Mean_Event_Delay_Days": float(np.mean(mean_delay)),\n                "P95_Event_Delay_Days": _pct(p95_delay, 0.95),\n                "Total_Shortage_Delay_Mean": float(\n                    pd.to_numeric(sub["Total_Shortage_Delay_Days"], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).mean()\n                ),\n            })\n\n    summary_df = pd.DataFrame(summary_rows)\n\n    part_rows: List[Dict[str, object]] = []\n    for months in periods:\n        for ai, aid in enumerate(alt_ids):\n            a = part_agg[(months, ai)]\n            for pi, pid in enumerate(part_ids):\n                d = a["demand"][pi]\n                sh = a["shortage"][pi]\n                imm = a["immediate"][pi]\n                part_rows.append({\n                    "Alternative_ID": aid,\n                    "Strategy": str(alt_meta.loc[aid, "Strategy"]),\n                    "Period_Months": months,\n                    "Part_ID": pid,\n                    "Initial_Stock": int(stock[ai, pi]),\n                    "Lambda_Annual_MC_Base": float(lam_y[pi]),\n                    "Replenishment_Cycle_H": float(cycle_h[pi]),\n                    "Repairable_Flag": int(repairable[pi]),\n                    "Demand_Mean": float(d / n_iter),\n                    "Shortage_Mean": float(sh / n_iter),\n                    "Service_Rate_Aggregated": 1.0 if d <= 0 else float(imm / d),\n                    "Mean_Shortage_Delay_Days": 0.0 if sh <= 0 else float(a["delay"][pi] / sh),\n                })\n    part_df = pd.DataFrame(part_rows)\n\n    config_df = pd.DataFrame([{\n        "Iterations": n_iter,\n        "Random_Seed": int(config.random_seed),\n        "Period_Months": ",".join(map(str, periods)),\n        "K_Distribution": config.k_distribution,\n        "K_Granularity": "PART x PERIOD x ITERATION",\n        "K_Median_Input": float(config.k_median),\n        "K_P05_Input": float(config.k_p05),\n        "K_P95_Input": float(config.k_p95),\n        "Common_Random_Numbers": bool(config.common_random_numbers),\n        "CRN_Shared_Across": "Representative alternatives for same part/period/iteration",\n        "Evaluation_Methods": "월 동일 가중 | 건수 가중 | 가중평균",\n        "Decision_Primary_Method": "건수 가중",\n        "Weighted_Blend_Alpha": float(config.weighted_blend_alpha),\n        "Replenishment_Rule": "Repairable=>Repair_TAT_H; NonRepairable=>Lead_Time_H",\n        "Historical_Data_Used": False,\n    }])\n\n    return {\n        "mc_summary_df": summary_df,\n        "mc_iteration_df": iteration_df,\n        "mc_part_summary_df": part_df,\n        "mc_config_df": config_df,\n        "mc_validation": {\n            "ready": True,\n            "alternative_count": len(alt_ids),\n            "part_count": len(part_ids),\n            "period_count": len(periods),\n            "method_count": len(method_map),\n            "iterations": n_iter,\n            "common_random_numbers": bool(config.common_random_numbers),\n        },\n    }\n\n\n__all__ = ["MonteCarloConfig", "validate_monte_carlo_config", "run_coldstart_monte_carlo"]\n'
_DECISION_ENGINE_SOURCE = '# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-\n"""\ndecision_engine.py\n==================\n\nFINAL DEBUGGED · Cost / Risk decision layer for Model-C + Cold-start Monte Carlo.\n\nDesign principle\n----------------\nThis module does NOT alter NSGA-II alternatives and does NOT rerun Monte Carlo.\nIt translates already-computed outputs into transparent decision-support indicators.\n\nNo hidden "best solution" is created.\nNo single weighted overall score is used.\n\nPrimary Monte Carlo comparison method\n-------------------------------------\nThe Decision Layer uses \'건수 가중\' (total immediate fulfilled / total demand)\nas the primary physical service comparison. Monthly-equal and blended results\nremain available in the simulation output for sensitivity review.\n\nThree independent decision axes\n-------------------------------\n1) Future Stability\n   Stability_Core = min(Service_P05, Probability_Zero_Shortage)\n\n   Rationale:\n   Both values are probabilities on the same 0~1 scale.\n   Using the minimum is deliberately non-compensatory: a very strong metric cannot\n   hide a weak one. If Stability_Core ties (common in sparse-demand systems),\n   Probability_Zero_Shortage is used only as a transparent tie-break.\n\n2) Shortage Risk\n   Primary physical indicator = Probability_Any_Shortage\n                              = 1 - Probability_Zero_Shortage\n   Secondary tie-break       = Shortage_P95\n\n3) Cost Efficiency\n   Baseline = minimum-stock representative alternative for the same period.\n              If more than one solution has the same minimum stock, lower cost is used.\n   Risk_Reduction_vs_MinStock = baseline Probability_Any_Shortage\n                                - alternative Probability_Any_Shortage\n   Additional_Cost_vs_MinStock = Cost - baseline Cost\n   Risk_Reduction_per_100M_KRW =\n       Risk_Reduction_vs_MinStock / (Additional_Cost_vs_MinStock / 100,000,000)\n\n   The minimum-stock baseline is labeled "기준" because an incremental efficiency\n   ratio is not mathematically defined when additional cost is zero. If another\n   inventory composition is cheaper and also lowers shortage risk, it is labeled\n   "비용감소" rather than forcing an invalid positive-cost efficiency grade.\n\nRelative grades\n---------------\nA/B/C/D/E are RELATIVE grades among the representative alternatives in the SAME\nsimulation period. They are not regulatory certification or absolute safety levels.\n\n- Future Stability: higher Stability_Core is better.\n- Shortage Risk: lower Probability_Any_Shortage is better; Shortage_P95 is tie-break.\n- Cost Efficiency: higher Risk_Reduction_per_100M_KRW is better among alternatives\n  with positive additional cost and positive risk reduction.\n\nGrades are based on deterministic sorted order with tolerance-aware ties.\nIf two values are numerically equal within tolerance, they receive the same grade.\n\nThe raw values and grade basis are always returned for audit.\n\nStage 6 interface\n-----------------\ndecision_table_df and audit metadata are consumed by audit_engine.py.\nAudit is read-only; no grade or decision result is recalculated by the audit layer.\n"""\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\n\n\nGRADE_LABELS = ["A", "B", "C", "D", "E"]\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass DecisionConfig:\n    decision_period_months: int = 36\n    primary_evaluation_method: str = "건수 가중"\n    tie_tolerance: float = 1e-9\n    cost_unit_krw: float = 100_000_000.0\n    grade_labels: Tuple[str, str, str, str, str] = ("A", "B", "C", "D", "E")\n\n\ndef validate_decision_config(config: DecisionConfig) -> Dict[str, object]:\n    issues: List[str] = []\n    if int(config.decision_period_months) <= 0:\n        issues.append("decision_period_months must be > 0.")\n    if not str(config.primary_evaluation_method).strip():\n        issues.append("primary_evaluation_method must be non-empty.")\n    if float(config.tie_tolerance) < 0:\n        issues.append("tie_tolerance must be >= 0.")\n    if float(config.cost_unit_krw) <= 0:\n        issues.append("cost_unit_krw must be > 0.")\n    if len(tuple(config.grade_labels)) != 5:\n        issues.append("grade_labels must contain exactly five labels.")\n    return {"ready": len(issues) == 0, "issues": issues}\n\n\ndef _require_columns(df: pd.DataFrame, required: List[str], name: str) -> None:\n    missing = [c for c in required if c not in df.columns]\n    if missing:\n        raise ValueError(f"{name} missing required columns: {missing}")\n\n\ndef _stable_relative_grades(\n    values: pd.Series,\n    higher_is_better: bool,\n    labels: Tuple[str, str, str, str, str],\n    tolerance: float,\n    secondary: Optional[pd.Series] = None,\n    secondary_higher_is_better: bool = False,\n) -> pd.Series:\n    """\n    Deterministic A~E relative grading with tolerance-aware ties.\n\n    Rank positions are mapped across available grade labels. For five distinct\n    alternatives this becomes A/B/C/D/E. When values tie within tolerance they\n    receive the same grade.\n    """\n    idx = values.index\n    primary = pd.to_numeric(values, errors="coerce")\n    if secondary is None:\n        secondary_num = pd.Series(0.0, index=idx)\n    else:\n        secondary_num = pd.to_numeric(secondary, errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)\n\n    valid = primary.notna()\n    if not valid.any():\n        return pd.Series(["-"] * len(values), index=idx, dtype=object)\n\n    work = pd.DataFrame({\n        "primary": primary,\n        "secondary": secondary_num,\n        "_idx": np.arange(len(idx)),\n    }, index=idx)\n    work = work.loc[valid].copy()\n\n    work = work.sort_values(\n        ["primary", "secondary", "_idx"],\n        ascending=[not higher_is_better, not secondary_higher_is_better, True],\n        kind="mergesort",\n    )\n\n    # Dense group number with tolerance-aware primary ties and secondary ties.\n    groups: List[int] = []\n    g = 0\n    prev_p = None\n    prev_s = None\n    for _, row in work.iterrows():\n        p = float(row["primary"])\n        s = float(row["secondary"])\n        if prev_p is None:\n            groups.append(g)\n        else:\n            same_p = abs(p - prev_p) <= tolerance\n            same_s = abs(s - prev_s) <= tolerance\n            if not (same_p and same_s):\n                g += 1\n            groups.append(g)\n        prev_p = p\n        prev_s = s\n    work["_group"] = groups\n\n    n_groups = int(work["_group"].max()) + 1 if len(work) else 1\n    label_list = list(labels)\n    group_to_label: Dict[int, str] = {}\n    for group in range(n_groups):\n        if n_groups <= 1:\n            label_idx = 0\n        else:\n            label_idx = int(round(group * (len(label_list) - 1) / (n_groups - 1)))\n        group_to_label[group] = label_list[min(max(label_idx, 0), len(label_list) - 1)]\n\n    out = pd.Series(["-"] * len(values), index=idx, dtype=object)\n    for row_idx, row in work.iterrows():\n        out.loc[row_idx] = group_to_label[int(row["_group"])]\n    return out\n\n\ndef _cost_efficiency_grades(\n    efficiency: pd.Series,\n    additional_cost: pd.Series,\n    risk_reduction: pd.Series,\n    labels: Tuple[str, str, str, str, str],\n    tolerance: float,\n) -> pd.Series:\n    out = pd.Series(["-"] * len(efficiency), index=efficiency.index, dtype=object)\n\n    base_mask = pd.to_numeric(additional_cost, errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).abs() <= tolerance\n    out.loc[base_mask] = "기준"\n\n    add_cost = pd.to_numeric(additional_cost, errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)\n    risk_red = pd.to_numeric(risk_reduction, errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)\n\n    # If a higher-stock composition is actually cheaper than the minimum-stock baseline\n    # and also reduces shortage risk, a positive incremental-cost efficiency ratio is\n    # not the right concept. Mark it transparently as cost-decreasing instead.\n    cost_decrease_gain = (~base_mask) & (add_cost < -tolerance) & (risk_red > tolerance)\n    out.loc[cost_decrease_gain] = "비용감소"\n\n    valid = (\n        ~base_mask\n        & (add_cost > tolerance)\n        & pd.to_numeric(efficiency, errors="coerce").notna()\n        & (risk_red > tolerance)\n    )\n    if valid.any():\n        out.loc[valid] = _stable_relative_grades(\n            pd.to_numeric(efficiency.loc[valid], errors="coerce"),\n            higher_is_better=True,\n            labels=labels,\n            tolerance=tolerance,\n        )\n\n    # Positive additional spending without risk reduction is explicitly E.\n    no_gain = (~base_mask) & (add_cost > tolerance) & (risk_red <= tolerance)\n    out.loc[no_gain] = labels[-1]\n\n    # Lower cost but not lower risk: no efficiency grade; show comparison only.\n    cost_decrease_no_gain = (~base_mask) & (add_cost < -tolerance) & (risk_red <= tolerance)\n    out.loc[cost_decrease_no_gain] = "비교"\n\n    return out\n\n\ndef build_decision_package(\n    representative_alternatives_df: pd.DataFrame,\n    mc_summary_df: pd.DataFrame,\n    config: Optional[DecisionConfig] = None,\n) -> Dict[str, object]:\n    if config is None:\n        config = DecisionConfig()\n\n    check = validate_decision_config(config)\n    if not check["ready"]:\n        raise ValueError("Invalid DecisionConfig: " + "; ".join(check["issues"]))\n\n    reps = representative_alternatives_df.copy()\n    mc = mc_summary_df.copy()\n\n    _require_columns(\n        reps,\n        ["Alternative_ID", "Strategy", "NSGA_Cost_KRW", "Stock_Units"],\n        "representative_alternatives_df",\n    )\n    _require_columns(\n        mc,\n        [\n            "Alternative_ID", "Strategy", "Period_Months",\n            "Service_P05", "Probability_Zero_Shortage",\n            "Shortage_P95", "Shortage_Mean", "Mean_Event_Delay_Days",\n        ],\n        "mc_summary_df",\n    )\n\n    reps["Alternative_ID"] = reps["Alternative_ID"].astype(str)\n    mc["Alternative_ID"] = mc["Alternative_ID"].astype(str)\n\n    periods = sorted(\n        pd.to_numeric(mc["Period_Months"], errors="coerce").dropna().astype(int).unique().tolist()\n    )\n    if not periods:\n        raise ValueError("mc_summary_df contains no valid Period_Months.")\n\n    requested_period = int(config.decision_period_months)\n    decision_period = requested_period if requested_period in periods else max(periods)\n\n    period_mask = (\n        pd.to_numeric(mc["Period_Months"], errors="coerce").astype(int) == decision_period\n    )\n    if "Evaluation_Method" in mc.columns:\n        method_mask = (\n            mc["Evaluation_Method"].astype(str)\n            == str(config.primary_evaluation_method)\n        )\n    else:\n        method_mask = pd.Series(True, index=mc.index)\n\n    period_mc = mc.loc[period_mask & method_mask].copy()\n    if period_mc.empty:\n        raise ValueError(\n            f"No Monte Carlo summary rows for period={decision_period}, "\n            f"method={config.primary_evaluation_method!r}."\n        )\n\n    merged = reps.merge(\n        period_mc,\n        on=["Alternative_ID", "Strategy"],\n        how="inner",\n        validate="one_to_one",\n    )\n    if merged.empty:\n        raise ValueError("No matching representative alternatives and Monte Carlo results.")\n\n    numeric_cols = [\n        "NSGA_Cost_KRW", "Stock_Units",\n        "Service_Mean", "Service_P05", "Probability_Zero_Shortage",\n        "Shortage_P95", "Shortage_Mean", "Mean_Event_Delay_Days",\n    ]\n    for c in numeric_cols:\n        merged[c] = pd.to_numeric(merged[c], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)\n\n    merged["Stability_Core"] = np.minimum(\n        merged["Service_P05"].clip(0.0, 1.0),\n        merged["Probability_Zero_Shortage"].clip(0.0, 1.0),\n    )\n    merged["Probability_Any_Shortage"] = (\n        1.0 - merged["Probability_Zero_Shortage"].clip(0.0, 1.0)\n    )\n\n    # Baseline is the minimum-stock representative strategy.\n    # If stock ties exist, use the lower-cost alternative deterministically.\n    baseline_idx = (\n        merged.sort_values(\n            ["Stock_Units", "NSGA_Cost_KRW", "Alternative_ID"],\n            ascending=[True, True, True],\n            kind="mergesort",\n        )\n        .index[0]\n    )\n    baseline_cost = float(merged.loc[baseline_idx, "NSGA_Cost_KRW"])\n    baseline_risk = float(merged.loc[baseline_idx, "Probability_Any_Shortage"])\n    baseline_alt = str(merged.loc[baseline_idx, "Alternative_ID"])\n\n    merged["Additional_Cost_vs_MinStock_KRW"] = (\n        merged["NSGA_Cost_KRW"] - baseline_cost\n    )\n    merged["Risk_Reduction_vs_MinStock"] = (\n        baseline_risk - merged["Probability_Any_Shortage"]\n    )\n\n    denom = merged["Additional_Cost_vs_MinStock_KRW"] / float(config.cost_unit_krw)\n    merged["Risk_Reduction_per_100M_KRW"] = np.where(\n        denom > float(config.tie_tolerance),\n        merged["Risk_Reduction_vs_MinStock"] / denom,\n        np.nan,\n    )\n\n    labels = tuple(config.grade_labels)\n    tol = float(config.tie_tolerance)\n\n    merged["Future_Stability_Grade"] = _stable_relative_grades(\n        merged["Stability_Core"],\n        higher_is_better=True,\n        labels=labels,\n        tolerance=tol,\n        secondary=merged["Probability_Zero_Shortage"],\n        secondary_higher_is_better=True,\n    )\n\n    merged["Shortage_Risk_Grade"] = _stable_relative_grades(\n        merged["Probability_Any_Shortage"],\n        higher_is_better=False,\n        labels=labels,\n        tolerance=tol,\n        secondary=merged["Shortage_P95"],\n        secondary_higher_is_better=False,\n    )\n\n    merged["Cost_Efficiency_Grade"] = _cost_efficiency_grades(\n        merged["Risk_Reduction_per_100M_KRW"],\n        merged["Additional_Cost_vs_MinStock_KRW"],\n        merged["Risk_Reduction_vs_MinStock"],\n        labels=labels,\n        tolerance=tol,\n    )\n\n    merged["Decision_Period_Months"] = int(decision_period)\n    merged["Decision_Evaluation_Method"] = str(config.primary_evaluation_method)\n    merged["Cost_Efficiency_Baseline_ID"] = baseline_alt\n\n    # Human-readable descriptions without recommending a winner.\n    merged["Decision_Interpretation"] = merged.apply(\n        lambda r: (\n            f"{r[\'Strategy\']} | 미래 안정성 상대등급 {r[\'Future_Stability_Grade\']} | "\n            f"부족위험 상대등급 {r[\'Shortage_Risk_Grade\']} | "\n            f"비용효율 상대등급 {r[\'Cost_Efficiency_Grade\']} | "\n            f"Service P05 {float(r[\'Service_P05\'])*100:.1f}% | "\n            f"재고부족 0회 확률 {float(r[\'Probability_Zero_Shortage\'])*100:.1f}%"\n        ),\n        axis=1,\n    )\n\n    decision_table_cols = [\n        "Alternative_ID", "Strategy", "Decision_Period_Months", "Decision_Evaluation_Method",\n        "Stock_Units", "NSGA_Cost_KRW",\n        "Service_Mean", "Service_P05", "Probability_Zero_Shortage",\n        "Stability_Core", "Future_Stability_Grade",\n        "Probability_Any_Shortage", "Shortage_P95", "Shortage_Mean",\n        "Shortage_Risk_Grade",\n        "Additional_Cost_vs_MinStock_KRW", "Risk_Reduction_vs_MinStock",\n        "Risk_Reduction_per_100M_KRW", "Cost_Efficiency_Grade",\n        "Mean_Event_Delay_Days",\n        "Cost_Efficiency_Baseline_ID",\n        "Decision_Interpretation",\n    ]\n    decision_table_df = merged[decision_table_cols].sort_values(\n        ["Stock_Units", "NSGA_Cost_KRW"],\n        ascending=[True, True],\n    ).reset_index(drop=True)\n\n    grade_audit_df = pd.DataFrame([\n        {\n            "Axis": "미래 안정성 상대등급",\n            "Raw_Metric": "Stability_Core = min(Service_P05, Probability_Zero_Shortage)",\n            "Direction": "높을수록 좋음",\n            "Grade_Method": "Stability_Core 우선, 동률 시 Probability_Zero_Shortage로만 tie-break; 동일하면 동일 등급",\n            "Absolute_Certification": False,\n        },\n        {\n            "Axis": "부족위험 상대등급",\n            "Raw_Metric": "Probability_Any_Shortage = 1 - Probability_Zero_Shortage; Shortage_P95 tie-break",\n            "Direction": "낮을수록 좋음",\n            "Grade_Method": "동일 기간 대표대안 간 상대 A~E, 수치 동률은 동일 등급",\n            "Absolute_Certification": False,\n        },\n        {\n            "Axis": "비용효율 상대등급",\n            "Raw_Metric": "Risk_Reduction_vs_MinStock / (Additional_Cost_vs_MinStock / 100M KRW)",\n            "Direction": "높을수록 좋음",\n            "Grade_Method": "최소비용안은 \'기준\'; 추가비용 대비 부족확률 감소가 있는 대안끼리 상대 A~E",\n            "Absolute_Certification": False,\n        },\n    ])\n\n    decision_config_df = pd.DataFrame([{\n        "Requested_Decision_Period_Months": requested_period,\n        "Applied_Decision_Period_Months": decision_period,\n        "Primary_Evaluation_Method": str(config.primary_evaluation_method),\n        "Tie_Tolerance": float(config.tie_tolerance),\n        "Cost_Unit_KRW": float(config.cost_unit_krw),\n        "Baseline_Alternative_ID": baseline_alt,\n        "Baseline_Type": "Minimum stock representative alternative",\n        "Baseline_Cost_KRW": baseline_cost,\n        "Baseline_Probability_Any_Shortage": baseline_risk,\n        "Overall_Weighted_Score_Used": False,\n        "Automatic_Best_Selection_Used": False,\n        "Grade_Type": "Relative descriptive grade within same period",\n    }])\n\n    # Positioning map data; no composite score.\n    positioning_df = decision_table_df[[\n        "Alternative_ID", "Strategy", "Stock_Units", "NSGA_Cost_KRW",\n        "Service_Mean", "Stability_Core", "Future_Stability_Grade",\n        "Probability_Any_Shortage", "Shortage_Risk_Grade",\n        "Risk_Reduction_per_100M_KRW", "Cost_Efficiency_Grade",\n    ]].copy()\n\n    return {\n        "decision_table_df": decision_table_df,\n        "decision_positioning_df": positioning_df,\n        "decision_grade_audit_df": grade_audit_df,\n        "decision_config_df": decision_config_df,\n        "decision_summary": {\n            "decision_period_months": int(decision_period),\n            "primary_evaluation_method": str(config.primary_evaluation_method),\n            "baseline_alternative_id": baseline_alt,\n            "alternative_count": int(len(decision_table_df)),\n            "overall_weighted_score_used": False,\n            "automatic_best_selection_used": False,\n        },\n    }\n\n\n__all__ = [\n    "DecisionConfig",\n    "validate_decision_config",\n    "build_decision_package",\n]\n'
_AUDIT_ENGINE_SOURCE = '# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-\n"""\naudit_engine.py\n===============\n\nPBL SUPPLY STRUCTURE OPTION · Final integration / audit / regression layer.\n\nPurpose\n-------\nThis module validates the integrity of the already-computed pipeline:\n\n    Model-C NSGA-II\n        -> Representative Bridge\n        -> Cold-start Monte Carlo\n        -> Cost/Risk Decision Layer\n\nIt does NOT modify or re-optimize any result.\n\nCore checks\n-----------\n1. Required output tables exist.\n2. Representative alternative IDs are unique.\n3. Representative Stock_Units reconcile with MC Bridge Stock_Qty sums.\n4. MC periods/iteration counts are structurally complete.\n5. Common Random Numbers are preserved across alternatives.\n6. Probability metrics are in [0, 1].\n7. Decision formulas reproduce from raw Monte Carlo metrics.\n8. Minimum-stock baseline is used for cost-efficiency.\n9. No overall weighted score / no automatic best selection.\n10. Decision grades remain descriptive/relative only.\n\nOutputs\n-------\naudit_summary_df\naudit_checks_df\naudit_strategy_df\naudit_part_risk_df\naudit_config_df\n"""\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom typing import Dict, List, Optional\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass AuditConfig:\n    probability_tolerance: float = 1e-9\n    numeric_tolerance: float = 1e-8\n    expected_periods: tuple[int, ...] = (12, 24, 36)\n\n\ndef validate_audit_config(config: AuditConfig) -> Dict[str, object]:\n    issues: List[str] = []\n    if float(config.probability_tolerance) < 0:\n        issues.append("probability_tolerance must be >= 0.")\n    if float(config.numeric_tolerance) < 0:\n        issues.append("numeric_tolerance must be >= 0.")\n    periods = tuple(int(x) for x in config.expected_periods)\n    if not periods or any(x <= 0 for x in periods):\n        issues.append("expected_periods must contain positive integers.")\n    return {"ready": len(issues) == 0, "issues": issues, "expected_periods": periods}\n\n\ndef _check_row(name: str, status: bool, detail: str, severity: str = "ERROR") -> Dict[str, object]:\n    return {\n        "Check": name,\n        "Status": "PASS" if bool(status) else "FAIL",\n        "Severity": severity,\n        "Detail": detail,\n    }\n\n\ndef _safe_df(result: dict, key: str) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    value = result.get(key, pd.DataFrame())\n    return value.copy() if isinstance(value, pd.DataFrame) else pd.DataFrame()\n\n\ndef build_final_audit_package(\n    result: dict,\n    config: Optional[AuditConfig] = None,\n) -> Dict[str, object]:\n    if config is None:\n        config = AuditConfig()\n\n    cfg_check = validate_audit_config(config)\n    if not cfg_check["ready"]:\n        raise ValueError("Invalid AuditConfig: " + "; ".join(cfg_check["issues"]))\n\n    checks: List[Dict[str, object]] = []\n    tol = float(config.numeric_tolerance)\n    ptol = float(config.probability_tolerance)\n    expected_periods = tuple(int(x) for x in config.expected_periods)\n\n    reps = _safe_df(result, "representative_alternatives_df")\n    bridge = _safe_df(result, "monte_carlo_bridge_df")\n    mc_summary = _safe_df(result, "mc_summary_df")\n    mc_iter = _safe_df(result, "mc_iteration_df")\n    mc_part = _safe_df(result, "mc_part_summary_df")\n    decision = _safe_df(result, "decision_table_df")\n    decision_audit = _safe_df(result, "decision_grade_audit_df")\n    decision_cfg = _safe_df(result, "decision_config_df")\n\n    required_tables = {\n        "representative_alternatives_df": reps,\n        "monte_carlo_bridge_df": bridge,\n        "mc_summary_df": mc_summary,\n        "mc_iteration_df": mc_iter,\n        "decision_table_df": decision,\n    }\n    for key, df in required_tables.items():\n        checks.append(_check_row(\n            f"required_table::{key}",\n            isinstance(df, pd.DataFrame) and not df.empty,\n            f"rows={len(df) if isinstance(df, pd.DataFrame) else 0}",\n        ))\n\n    # Representative IDs\n    rep_unique_ok = (\n        not reps.empty\n        and "Alternative_ID" in reps.columns\n        and reps["Alternative_ID"].astype(str).is_unique\n    )\n    checks.append(_check_row(\n        "representative_id_unique",\n        rep_unique_ok,\n        f"representative_count={len(reps)}",\n    ))\n\n    # Bridge stock reconciliation\n    bridge_stock_ok = True\n    bridge_stock_details = []\n    if reps.empty or bridge.empty or not {"Alternative_ID", "Stock_Units"}.issubset(reps.columns) \\\n            or not {"Alternative_ID", "Stock_Qty"}.issubset(bridge.columns):\n        bridge_stock_ok = False\n        bridge_stock_details.append("required columns missing")\n    else:\n        for _, r in reps.iterrows():\n            aid = str(r["Alternative_ID"])\n            expected_stock = int(round(float(pd.to_numeric(r["Stock_Units"], errors="coerce") or 0)))\n            actual_stock = int(\n                pd.to_numeric(\n                    bridge.loc[bridge["Alternative_ID"].astype(str) == aid, "Stock_Qty"],\n                    errors="coerce",\n                ).fillna(0).sum()\n            )\n            ok = expected_stock == actual_stock\n            bridge_stock_ok = bridge_stock_ok and ok\n            bridge_stock_details.append(f"{aid}:{actual_stock}/{expected_stock}")\n    checks.append(_check_row(\n        "bridge_stock_reconciliation",\n        bridge_stock_ok,\n        " | ".join(bridge_stock_details[:12]),\n    ))\n\n    supply_stock_ok = False\n    supply_detail = []\n    required_supply_cols = {\n        "Stock_O", "Stock_I", "Stock_D", "Stock_Qty",\n        "Maint_Echelon", "Stocking_Echelon", "Supply_Structure_Mode"\n    }\n    if not bridge.empty and required_supply_cols.issubset(bridge.columns):\n        so = pd.to_numeric(bridge["Stock_O"], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)\n        si = pd.to_numeric(bridge["Stock_I"], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)\n        sd = pd.to_numeric(bridge["Stock_D"], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)\n        sq = pd.to_numeric(bridge["Stock_Qty"], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)\n\n        maint = bridge["Maint_Echelon"].astype(str).str.upper().str.strip()\n        stocking = bridge["Stocking_Echelon"].astype(str).str.upper().str.strip()\n        modes = bridge["Supply_Structure_Mode"].astype(str).str.upper().str.strip()\n\n        mode_values = sorted(modes.dropna().unique().tolist())\n        one_mode_ok = len(mode_values) == 1\n        mode = mode_values[0] if one_mode_ok else ""\n\n        total_ok = bool(np.allclose(so + si + sd, sq, atol=tol, rtol=0.0))\n\n        if mode == "MAINT_ECHELON":\n            stocking_rule_ok = bool((stocking == maint.where(maint.isin(["O","I","D"]), "D")).all())\n            o_ok = bool(((stocking != "O") | ((si.abs() <= tol) & (sd.abs() <= tol))).all())\n            i_ok = bool(((stocking != "I") | ((so.abs() <= tol) & (sd.abs() <= tol))).all())\n            d_ok = bool(((stocking != "D") | ((so.abs() <= tol) & (si.abs() <= tol))).all())\n            supply_stock_ok = bool(one_mode_ok and total_ok and stocking_rule_ok and o_ok and i_ok and d_ok)\n            supply_detail = [\n                f"mode={mode}",\n                f"total_equals_qty={total_ok}",\n                f"stocking_matches_maint={stocking_rule_ok}",\n                f"O_stock_alignment={o_ok}",\n                f"I_stock_alignment={i_ok}",\n                f"D_stock_alignment={d_ok}",\n            ]\n\n        elif mode == "DEPOT_DIRECT":\n            stocking_rule_ok = bool((stocking == "D").all())\n            o_zero = bool((so.abs() <= tol).all())\n            i_zero = bool((si.abs() <= tol).all())\n            d_equals_total = bool(np.allclose(sd, sq, atol=tol, rtol=0.0))\n            supply_stock_ok = bool(\n                one_mode_ok and total_ok and stocking_rule_ok\n                and o_zero and i_zero and d_equals_total\n            )\n            supply_detail = [\n                f"mode={mode}",\n                f"total_equals_qty={total_ok}",\n                f"all_stocking_echelon_D={stocking_rule_ok}",\n                f"Stock_O_zero={o_zero}",\n                f"Stock_I_zero={i_zero}",\n                f"Stock_D_equals_total={d_equals_total}",\n            ]\n        else:\n            supply_detail = [\n                f"mode_values={mode_values}",\n                "unsupported_or_mixed_supply_structure_mode",\n            ]\n\n    checks.append(_check_row(\n        "pbl_supply_structure_stock_alignment",\n        supply_stock_ok,\n        " | ".join(supply_detail) if supply_detail else "required columns missing",\n    ))\n\n    bridge_lambda_ok = False\n    if not bridge.empty and {\n        "Failure_Rate", "Annual_Operating_Hours", "QPA", "Lambda_Annual_MC_Base"\n    }.issubset(bridge.columns):\n        fr = pd.to_numeric(bridge["Failure_Rate"], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).to_numpy(float)\n        oh = pd.to_numeric(bridge["Annual_Operating_Hours"], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).to_numpy(float)\n        qpa = pd.to_numeric(bridge["QPA"], errors="coerce").fillna(1.0).to_numpy(float)\n        lam = pd.to_numeric(bridge["Lambda_Annual_MC_Base"], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).to_numpy(float)\n        expected_lam = fr * oh / 1_000_000.0 * qpa\n        bridge_lambda_ok = bool(np.allclose(expected_lam, lam, atol=tol, rtol=1e-10))\n    checks.append(_check_row(\n        "mc_bridge_lambda_consistency",\n        bridge_lambda_ok,\n        "Lambda_Annual_MC_Base == Failure_Rate × Annual_Operating_Hours / 1e6 × QPA",\n    ))\n\n    # MC summary period coverage.\n    periods_actual = set()\n    if not mc_summary.empty and "Period_Months" in mc_summary.columns:\n        periods_actual = set(\n            pd.to_numeric(mc_summary["Period_Months"], errors="coerce").dropna().astype(int).tolist()\n        )\n    period_ok = set(expected_periods).issubset(periods_actual)\n    checks.append(_check_row(\n        "mc_period_coverage",\n        period_ok,\n        f"expected={list(expected_periods)}, actual={sorted(periods_actual)}",\n    ))\n\n    # Summary structural completeness: alternatives x periods x evaluation methods.\n    alt_count = int(reps["Alternative_ID"].nunique()) if not reps.empty and "Alternative_ID" in reps.columns else 0\n    if "Evaluation_Method" in mc_summary.columns:\n        methods_actual = sorted(mc_summary["Evaluation_Method"].astype(str).dropna().unique().tolist())\n    else:\n        methods_actual = []\n    expected_methods = ["가중평균", "건수 가중", "월 동일 가중"]\n    methods_ok = set(expected_methods).issubset(set(methods_actual))\n    checks.append(_check_row(\n        "mc_evaluation_methods",\n        methods_ok,\n        f"expected={expected_methods}, actual={methods_actual}",\n    ))\n\n    expected_summary_rows = alt_count * len(expected_periods) * len(expected_methods)\n    summary_shape_ok = len(mc_summary) == expected_summary_rows if expected_summary_rows > 0 else False\n    checks.append(_check_row(\n        "mc_summary_shape",\n        summary_shape_ok,\n        f"actual={len(mc_summary)}, expected={expected_summary_rows}",\n    ))\n\n    # Probability bounds.\n    probability_cols = ["Service_Mean", "Service_P05", "Probability_Zero_Shortage"]\n    prob_ok = True\n    prob_detail = []\n    for col in probability_cols:\n        if col not in mc_summary.columns:\n            prob_ok = False\n            prob_detail.append(f"{col}:missing")\n            continue\n        v = pd.to_numeric(mc_summary[col], errors="coerce")\n        ok = v.notna().all() and ((v >= -ptol) & (v <= 1.0 + ptol)).all()\n        prob_ok = prob_ok and bool(ok)\n        prob_detail.append(f"{col}:{\'OK\' if ok else \'BAD\'}")\n    checks.append(_check_row(\n        "mc_probability_bounds",\n        prob_ok,\n        ", ".join(prob_detail),\n    ))\n\n    # CRN integrity.\n    # K is part-specific, so iteration output stores Mean_K/P95_K summaries.\n    # These summaries and total physical demand must be identical across alternatives.\n    crn_k_ok = False\n    crn_demand_ok = False\n    if not mc_iter.empty and {\n        "Period_Months", "Iteration", "Mean_K", "P95_K", "Demand_Total"\n    }.issubset(mc_iter.columns):\n        g = mc_iter.groupby(["Period_Months", "Iteration"], dropna=False).agg(\n            MeanK_nunique=("Mean_K", "nunique"),\n            P95K_nunique=("P95_K", "nunique"),\n            Demand_nunique=("Demand_Total", "nunique"),\n        )\n        crn_k_ok = bool(\n            (g["MeanK_nunique"] <= 1).all()\n            and (g["P95K_nunique"] <= 1).all()\n        )\n        crn_demand_ok = bool((g["Demand_nunique"] <= 1).all())\n    checks.append(_check_row(\n        "mc_common_random_numbers_K",\n        crn_k_ok,\n        "same period/iteration must share identical K across alternatives",\n    ))\n    checks.append(_check_row(\n        "mc_common_random_numbers_demand",\n        crn_demand_ok,\n        "same period/iteration must share identical Demand_Total across alternatives",\n    ))\n\n    # Decision formula checks.\n    stability_ok = False\n    shortage_prob_ok = False\n    baseline_ok = False\n    no_auto_best_ok = False\n    no_weighted_ok = False\n\n    if not decision.empty:\n        if {"Service_P05", "Probability_Zero_Shortage", "Stability_Core"}.issubset(decision.columns):\n            expected_stability = np.minimum(\n                pd.to_numeric(decision["Service_P05"], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).to_numpy(float),\n                pd.to_numeric(decision["Probability_Zero_Shortage"], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).to_numpy(float),\n            )\n            actual_stability = pd.to_numeric(\n                decision["Stability_Core"], errors="coerce"\n            ).fillna(0.0).to_numpy(float)\n            stability_ok = bool(np.allclose(expected_stability, actual_stability, atol=tol, rtol=0.0))\n\n        if {"Probability_Zero_Shortage", "Probability_Any_Shortage"}.issubset(decision.columns):\n            expected_risk = 1.0 - pd.to_numeric(\n                decision["Probability_Zero_Shortage"], errors="coerce"\n            ).fillna(0.0).to_numpy(float)\n            actual_risk = pd.to_numeric(\n                decision["Probability_Any_Shortage"], errors="coerce"\n            ).fillna(0.0).to_numpy(float)\n            shortage_prob_ok = bool(np.allclose(expected_risk, actual_risk, atol=tol, rtol=0.0))\n\n        if {"Stock_Units", "Alternative_ID", "Cost_Efficiency_Baseline_ID"}.issubset(decision.columns):\n            min_stock = float(pd.to_numeric(decision["Stock_Units"], errors="coerce").min())\n            baseline_id = str(decision["Cost_Efficiency_Baseline_ID"].iloc[0])\n            hit = decision[decision["Alternative_ID"].astype(str) == baseline_id]\n            baseline_ok = (\n                not hit.empty\n                and abs(float(pd.to_numeric(hit["Stock_Units"], errors="coerce").iloc[0]) - min_stock) <= tol\n            )\n\n    checks.append(_check_row(\n        "decision_stability_formula",\n        stability_ok,\n        "Stability_Core == min(Service_P05, Probability_Zero_Shortage)",\n    ))\n    checks.append(_check_row(\n        "decision_shortage_probability_formula",\n        shortage_prob_ok,\n        "Probability_Any_Shortage == 1 - Probability_Zero_Shortage",\n    ))\n    checks.append(_check_row(\n        "decision_min_stock_baseline",\n        baseline_ok,\n        "cost-efficiency baseline must be minimum-stock representative alternative",\n    ))\n\n    decision_method_ok = False\n    if not decision.empty and "Decision_Evaluation_Method" in decision.columns:\n        decision_method_ok = bool(\n            (decision["Decision_Evaluation_Method"].astype(str) == "건수 가중").all()\n        )\n    checks.append(_check_row(\n        "decision_primary_method_case_weighted",\n        decision_method_ok,\n        "Decision Layer must use 건수 가중 as the primary physical comparison",\n    ))\n\n    if not decision_cfg.empty:\n        if "Automatic_Best_Selection_Used" in decision_cfg.columns:\n            no_auto_best_ok = bool(\n                (decision_cfg["Automatic_Best_Selection_Used"].astype(bool) == False).all()\n            )\n        if "Overall_Weighted_Score_Used" in decision_cfg.columns:\n            no_weighted_ok = bool(\n                (decision_cfg["Overall_Weighted_Score_Used"].astype(bool) == False).all()\n            )\n    checks.append(_check_row(\n        "decision_no_automatic_best",\n        no_auto_best_ok,\n        "automatic best solution selection must remain disabled",\n    ))\n    checks.append(_check_row(\n        "decision_no_weighted_overall_score",\n        no_weighted_ok,\n        "single weighted overall score must remain disabled",\n    ))\n\n    relative_grade_ok = False\n    if not decision_audit.empty and "Absolute_Certification" in decision_audit.columns:\n        relative_grade_ok = bool(\n            (decision_audit["Absolute_Certification"].astype(bool) == False).all()\n        )\n    checks.append(_check_row(\n        "decision_grades_are_relative",\n        relative_grade_ok,\n        "A~E grades must be descriptive/relative, not absolute certification",\n    ))\n\n    audit_checks_df = pd.DataFrame(checks)\n    total_checks = int(len(audit_checks_df))\n    pass_count = int((audit_checks_df["Status"] == "PASS").sum()) if total_checks else 0\n    fail_count = total_checks - pass_count\n    overall_status = "PASS" if fail_count == 0 else "CHECK"\n\n    audit_summary_df = pd.DataFrame([{\n        "Overall_Status": overall_status,\n        "Total_Checks": total_checks,\n        "Pass_Count": pass_count,\n        "Fail_Count": fail_count,\n        "Representative_Alternatives": alt_count,\n        "MC_Periods": ",".join(map(str, sorted(periods_actual))),\n        "Decision_Alternatives": int(len(decision)),\n        "Automatic_Best_Selection": False,\n        "Weighted_Overall_Score": False,\n    }])\n\n    # Strategy-level final consolidated view.\n    if not decision.empty:\n        strategy_cols = [c for c in [\n            "Alternative_ID", "Strategy", "Decision_Period_Months",\n            "Stock_Units", "NSGA_Cost_KRW",\n            "Service_P05", "Probability_Zero_Shortage",\n            "Shortage_P95", "Mean_Event_Delay_Days",\n            "Future_Stability_Grade", "Shortage_Risk_Grade",\n            "Cost_Efficiency_Grade",\n        ] if c in decision.columns]\n        audit_strategy_df = decision[strategy_cols].copy()\n    else:\n        audit_strategy_df = pd.DataFrame()\n\n    # Part-level final risk drill-down.\n    if not mc_part.empty:\n        part = mc_part.copy()\n        for c in ["Shortage_Mean", "Demand_Mean", "Mean_Shortage_Delay_Days"]:\n            if c not in part.columns:\n                part[c] = 0.0\n            part[c] = pd.to_numeric(part[c], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)\n        part["Risk_Priority_Index"] = (\n            part["Shortage_Mean"]\n            * (1.0 + part["Mean_Shortage_Delay_Days"])\n        )\n        audit_part_risk_df = part.sort_values(\n            ["Period_Months", "Risk_Priority_Index", "Demand_Mean"],\n            ascending=[True, False, False],\n        ).reset_index(drop=True)\n    else:\n        audit_part_risk_df = pd.DataFrame()\n\n    audit_config_df = pd.DataFrame([{\n        "Expected_Periods": ",".join(map(str, expected_periods)),\n        "Probability_Tolerance": ptol,\n        "Numeric_Tolerance": tol,\n        "Audit_Changes_Analysis_Results": False,\n        "Audit_Only": True,\n    }])\n\n    return {\n        "audit_summary_df": audit_summary_df,\n        "audit_checks_df": audit_checks_df,\n        "audit_strategy_df": audit_strategy_df,\n        "audit_part_risk_df": audit_part_risk_df,\n        "audit_config_df": audit_config_df,\n        "audit_summary": {\n            "overall_status": overall_status,\n            "total_checks": total_checks,\n            "pass_count": pass_count,\n            "fail_count": fail_count,\n        },\n    }\n\n\n__all__ = [\n    "AuditConfig",\n    "validate_audit_config",\n    "build_final_audit_package",\n]\n'


def _load_embedded_module(name: str, source: str):
    import sys as _sys
    mod = _types.ModuleType(name)
    mod.__dict__["__name__"] = name
    _sys.modules[name] = mod
    exec(compile(source, f"<embedded {name}>", "exec"), mod.__dict__)
    return mod


_sim = _load_embedded_module("embedded_simulation_engine", _SIMULATION_ENGINE_SOURCE)
_dec = _load_embedded_module("embedded_decision_engine", _DECISION_ENGINE_SOURCE)
_aud = _load_embedded_module("embedded_audit_engine", _AUDIT_ENGINE_SOURCE)


def _read_input(path: str, sheet=None):
    p = _Path(path)
    if not p.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Input file not found: {p}")
    s = p.suffix.lower()
    if s in (".xlsx", ".xlsm"):
        return pd.read_excel(p, sheet_name=(0 if sheet in (None, "") else sheet), engine="openpyxl")
    if s == ".xls":
        return pd.read_excel(p, sheet_name=(0 if sheet in (None, "") else sheet))
    if s == ".csv":
        errs = []
        for enc in ("utf-8-sig", "cp949", "euc-kr", "utf-8"):
            try:
                return pd.read_csv(p, encoding=enc)
            except Exception as e:
                errs.append(f"{enc}: {e}")
        raise RuntimeError("CSV read failed.\n" + "\n".join(errs))
    raise ValueError("Supported: .xlsx, .xlsm, .xls, .csv")


def _safe_sheet(name, used):
    import re
    s = re.sub(r'[:\\/?*\[\]]', "_", str(name))[:31] or "Sheet"
    base = s
    n = 2
    while s in used:
        suf = f"_{n}"
        s = base[:31-len(suf)] + suf
        n += 1
    used.add(s)
    return s


def _summary_df(d):
    rows = []
    if not isinstance(d, dict):
        d = {"Value": d}
    for k, v in d.items():
        if isinstance(v, (dict, list, tuple, set)):
            try:
                v = _json.dumps(v, ensure_ascii=False)
            except Exception:
                v = str(v)
        rows.append({"Key": str(k), "Value": v})
    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


def _progress_every(n=10):
    def cb(gen, total_gen, payload):
        event = str(payload.get("event", ""))
        if event == "stage_start":
            print(
                f"\n[Target {payload.get('sweep_stage_idx','?')}/"
                f"{payload.get('sweep_stage_total','?')}] "
                f"Ao={float(payload.get('current_target_ao',0)):.4f} 시작",
                flush=True,
            )
            return
        if event != "generation":
            return
        if gen == 1 or gen == total_gen or (n > 0 and gen % n == 0):
            parts = [f"GEN {int(gen):>4}/{int(total_gen)}"]
            for key, label, fmt in [
                ("best_Ao", "Ao", ".6f"),
                ("best_cost", "Cost", ",.0f"),
                ("best_tail_risk", "Tail", ".6f"),
            ]:
                val = payload.get(key)
                if val is not None:
                    try:
                        parts.append(f"{label}={float(val):{fmt}}")
                    except Exception:
                        pass
            print(" | ".join(parts), flush=True)
    return cb


def _build_dashboard_summary(nsga_result, mc_result, decision_result, audit_result, cfg, mc_cfg, decision_cfg):
    s = dict(nsga_result.get("summary", {}))
    s.update({
        "monte_carlo_iterations": int(mc_cfg.iterations),
        "decision_period_months": int(decision_cfg.decision_period_months),
        "representative_alternative_count": int(len(nsga_result.get("representative_alternatives_df", pd.DataFrame()))),
        "audit_status": audit_result.get("audit_summary", {}).get("overall_status", "-"),
        "audit_pass_count": audit_result.get("audit_summary", {}).get("pass_count", 0),
        "audit_fail_count": audit_result.get("audit_summary", {}).get("fail_count", 0),
        "supply_structure_mode": normalize_supply_structure_mode(cfg),
        "ao_policy_architecture_step": "STEP5_MATRIX_READY",
        "primary_knee_available": bool(
            isinstance(nsga_result.get("knee_summary_df"), pd.DataFrame)
            and not nsga_result.get("knee_summary_df").empty
            and (
                nsga_result.get("knee_summary_df")
                .get("Knee_Type", pd.Series(dtype=object))
                .astype(str)
                .eq("PRIMARY_COST_AO")
                .any()
            )
        ),
        "strategy_matrix_available_cells": int(
            (
                nsga_result.get(
                    "strategy_matrix_long_df",
                    pd.DataFrame(),
                )
                .get("Cell_Status", pd.Series(dtype=object))
                .astype(str)
                != "EMPTY"
            ).sum()
        ) if isinstance(
            nsga_result.get("strategy_matrix_long_df"),
            pd.DataFrame,
        ) else 0,
    })
    return s


def _excel_safe_value(v):
    """Convert arbitrary/numpy/container values to Excel-safe scalar values."""
    import math
    import json as _json_local

    if v is None:
        return None

    # numpy scalar -> native Python scalar
    try:
        if isinstance(v, np.generic):
            v = v.item()
    except Exception:
        pass

    if isinstance(v, (dict, list, tuple, set)):
        try:
            return _json_local.dumps(
                list(v) if isinstance(v, set) else v,
                ensure_ascii=False,
                default=str,
            )
        except Exception:
            return str(v)

    # pandas / numpy arrays and other iterable objects
    if isinstance(v, np.ndarray):
        try:
            return _json_local.dumps(v.tolist(), ensure_ascii=False, default=str)
        except Exception:
            return str(v)

    if isinstance(v, float):
        if not math.isfinite(v):
            return None
        return float(v)

    if isinstance(v, (str, int, bool)):
        return v

    # pandas timestamps/datetime-like values are generally Excel-safe.
    try:
        if pd.isna(v):
            return None
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Keep common datetime/date values; stringify unknown object types.
    try:
        import datetime as _dt
        if isinstance(v, (_dt.datetime, _dt.date, _dt.time)):
            return v
    except Exception:
        pass

    return str(v)


def _excel_safe_dataframe(df):
    """
    Make a dataframe safe for Excel serialization.
    Prevents nested Python objects, numpy arrays, +/-inf and unsupported objects
    from leaving a half-written workbook.
    """
    if df is None:
        return None
    if isinstance(df, dict):
        df = _summary_df(df)
    if not isinstance(df, pd.DataFrame):
        try:
            df = pd.DataFrame(df)
        except Exception:
            return None

    out = df.copy()

    # Excel cannot store infinities reliably; blank them.
    try:
        out = out.replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan)
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Only object columns need arbitrary-object normalization.
    for c in out.columns:
        if str(out[c].dtype) == "object":
            out[c] = out[c].map(_excel_safe_value)

    # Ensure column labels are simple strings.
    out.columns = [str(c) for c in out.columns]
    return out


def _verify_xlsx_file(path):
    """
    Verify XLSX physical integrity before publishing it as the final result.
    XLSX is a ZIP package; then openpyxl re-opens it as an Excel workbook.
    """
    import zipfile as _zipfile
    from openpyxl import load_workbook as _load_workbook

    p = _Path(path)
    if not p.exists():
        raise RuntimeError(f"XLSX verification failed: file was not created: {p}")
    if p.stat().st_size < 1000:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"XLSX verification failed: file is unexpectedly small ({p.stat().st_size} bytes)."
        )
    if not _zipfile.is_zipfile(p):
        raise RuntimeError("XLSX verification failed: output is not a valid ZIP/OOXML package.")

    with _zipfile.ZipFile(p, "r") as zf:
        bad_member = zf.testzip()
        if bad_member is not None:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"XLSX verification failed: damaged ZIP member: {bad_member}"
            )
        names = set(zf.namelist())
        required = {
            "[Content_Types].xml",
            "_rels/.rels",
            "xl/workbook.xml",
            "xl/_rels/workbook.xml.rels",
        }
        missing = sorted(required - names)
        if missing:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "XLSX verification failed: required OOXML entries missing: "
                + ", ".join(missing)
            )

    # Logical workbook-level validation.
    wb = _load_workbook(p, read_only=True, data_only=False)
    try:
        if not wb.sheetnames:
            raise RuntimeError("XLSX verification failed: workbook has no worksheets.")
    finally:
        wb.close()

    return True


def _export_dashboard_workbook(
    output_path, input_path,
    nsga_result, mc_result, decision_result, audit_result,
    cfg, mc_cfg, decision_cfg
):
    """
    Atomic and verified Excel export.

    IMPORTANT:
    - Writes to a temporary .xlsx first.
    - Verifies the OOXML ZIP package and re-opens it with openpyxl.
    - Only then replaces the final output file.
    - If export/verification fails, the temporary file is removed and an
      existing final result is left untouched.
    """
    import os as _os

    out = _Path(output_path)
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # Keep the temporary file in the same directory so os.replace is atomic
    # on ordinary Windows/local filesystems.
    tmp = out.with_name(f".{out.stem}.__writing__.xlsx")
    if tmp.exists():
        try:
            tmp.unlink()
        except Exception:
            pass

    dashboard_summary = _build_dashboard_summary(
        nsga_result, mc_result, decision_result, audit_result,
        cfg, mc_cfg, decision_cfg
    )

    run_config = pd.DataFrame([
        {"Setting":"Input_File","Value":str(input_path)},
        {"Setting":"Supply_Structure","Value":normalize_supply_structure_mode(cfg)},
        {"Setting":"Target_Ao","Value":float(cfg.target_ao)},
        {"Setting":"Representative_Target","Value":float(cfg.representative_target)},
        {"Setting":"Analysis_Years","Value":float(cfg.years)},
        {"Setting":"Population","Value":int(cfg.population_size)},
        {"Setting":"Generations","Value":int(cfg.n_generations)},
        {"Setting":"Seed","Value":int(cfg.random_seed)},
        {"Setting":"Pmin","Value":float(cfg.pmin)},
        {"Setting":"Long_Lead_Percentile","Value":float(cfg.long_lead_percentile)},
        {"Setting":"Ao_Impact_Percentile","Value":float(cfg.ao_impact_percentile)},
        {"Setting":"Future_Simulation_Iterations","Value":int(mc_cfg.iterations)},
        {"Setting":"Future_Simulation_Periods","Value":",".join(map(str, mc_cfg.period_months))},
        {"Setting":"K_Distribution","Value":"LogNormal"},
        {"Setting":"K_P05","Value":float(mc_cfg.k_p05)},
        {"Setting":"K_Median","Value":float(mc_cfg.k_median)},
        {"Setting":"K_P95","Value":float(mc_cfg.k_p95)},
        {"Setting":"Decision_Period","Value":int(decision_cfg.decision_period_months)},
        {"Setting":"Decision_Method","Value":str(decision_cfg.primary_evaluation_method)},
    ])

    tables = [
        ("Dashboard_Summary", _summary_df(dashboard_summary)),
        ("Run_Config", run_config),

        # Integrated NSGA results
        ("Prepared_Data", nsga_result.get("prepared_df")),
        ("Candidates", nsga_result.get("candidate_df")),
        ("Pareto", nsga_result.get("pareto_df")),
        ("Policy", nsga_result.get("policy_df")),
        ("Sweep", nsga_result.get("sweep_summary_df")),
        ("All_Target_Runs", nsga_result.get("all_target_runs_df")),
        ("Robust_Summary", nsga_result.get("robust_scenario_summary_df")),
        ("Robust_Detail", nsga_result.get("robust_scenario_detail_df")),
        ("Representatives", nsga_result.get("representative_alternatives_df")),
        ("Rep_Inventory", nsga_result.get("representative_inventory_df")),
        ("Future_Bridge", nsga_result.get("monte_carlo_bridge_df")),
        ("Rep_Selection_Audit", nsga_result.get("representative_selection_audit_df")),
        ("Ao_Band_Config", nsga_result.get("ao_policy_band_config_df")),
        ("Ao_Band_Summary", nsga_result.get("ao_policy_band_summary_df")),
        ("Band_Rep_Selection", nsga_result.get("band_selection_summary_df")),
        ("Policy_Anchor", nsga_result.get("policy_anchor_df")),
        ("Policy_Region_Summary", nsga_result.get("policy_region_summary_df")),

        # Ao policy architecture STEP 4
        ("Knee_Summary", nsga_result.get("knee_summary_df")),
        ("Band_Marginal_Risk", decision_result.get("band_marginal_risk_df")),

        # Ao policy architecture STEP 5
        ("Strategy_Matrix_Long", nsga_result.get("strategy_matrix_long_df")),
        ("Strategy_Matrix_View", nsga_result.get("strategy_matrix_view_df")),
        ("Strategy_Matrix_Metric", nsga_result.get("strategy_matrix_metric_df")),

        # Future simulation
        ("Future_Summary", mc_result.get("mc_summary_df")),
        ("Future_Iteration", mc_result.get("mc_iteration_df")),
        ("Future_Part_Summary", mc_result.get("mc_part_summary_df")),
        ("Future_Config", mc_result.get("mc_config_df")),

        # Cost / Risk decision
        ("Decision_Table", decision_result.get("decision_table_df")),
        ("Decision_Positioning", decision_result.get("decision_positioning_df")),
        ("Decision_Grade_Audit", decision_result.get("decision_grade_audit_df")),
        ("Decision_Config", decision_result.get("decision_config_df")),

        # Audit
        ("Audit_Summary", audit_result.get("audit_summary_df")),
        ("Audit_Checks", audit_result.get("audit_checks_df")),
        ("Audit_STEP7", audit_result.get("audit_step7_checks_df")),
        ("Audit_Strategy", audit_result.get("audit_strategy_df")),
        ("Audit_Part_Risk", audit_result.get("audit_part_risk_df")),
        ("Audit_Config", audit_result.get("audit_config_df")),
    ]

    used = set()

    try:
        # xlsx is written completely to TMP first.
        with pd.ExcelWriter(tmp, engine="openpyxl") as writer:
            written_sheet_count = 0
            for name, obj in tables:
                obj = _excel_safe_dataframe(obj)
                if obj is None:
                    continue
                if obj.empty and len(obj.columns) == 0:
                    continue

                sheet_name = _safe_sheet(name, used)
                obj.to_excel(writer, sheet_name=sheet_name, index=False)
                written_sheet_count += 1

            if written_sheet_count == 0:
                raise RuntimeError("No result sheets were available for Excel export.")

        # Physical + logical validation before final publication.
        _verify_xlsx_file(tmp)

        # Replace the final result only after verification succeeded.
        _os.replace(str(tmp), str(out))

        # Verify the published final path one more time.
        _verify_xlsx_file(out)

        print(
            f"[EXCEL] 저장 검증 PASS | "
            f"{out.name} | {out.stat().st_size:,} bytes",
            flush=True,
        )
        return out

    except Exception:
        try:
            if tmp.exists():
                tmp.unlink()
        except Exception:
            pass
        raise


def _dash_num(v, default=0.0):
    try:
        x = float(v)
        return x if np.isfinite(x) else default
    except Exception:
        return default


def _dash_esc(v):
    import html
    return html.escape(str(v), quote=True)


def _dash_scale(vals, out0, out1, pad=0.05):
    a = np.asarray(vals, dtype=float)
    a = a[np.isfinite(a)]
    if a.size == 0:
        return (lambda x: (out0 + out1) / 2.0), (0.0, 1.0)
    lo, hi = float(a.min()), float(a.max())
    if abs(hi - lo) < 1e-12:
        d = max(abs(lo), 1.0)
        lo -= d * 0.5
        hi += d * 0.5
    p = (hi - lo) * pad
    lo -= p
    hi += p
    return (lambda x: out0 + (float(x) - lo) / (hi - lo) * (out1 - out0)), (lo, hi)


def _dash_knee_band(cost, effect):
    """Reference band only; never selects an optimum."""
    x = np.asarray(cost, dtype=float)
    y = np.asarray(effect, dtype=float)
    good = np.isfinite(x) & np.isfinite(y)
    x, y = x[good], y[good]
    if len(x) < 4:
        return None
    order = np.argsort(x)
    x, y = x[order], y[order]
    ux, uy = [], []
    for xv in np.unique(x):
        ux.append(xv)
        uy.append(np.nanmax(y[x == xv]))
    x, y = np.asarray(ux, float), np.asarray(uy, float)
    if len(x) < 4:
        return None
    xr = max(x.max() - x.min(), 1e-12)
    yr = max(y.max() - y.min(), 1e-12)
    xn = (x - x.min()) / xr
    yn = np.maximum.accumulate((y - y.min()) / yr)
    dx, dy = np.diff(xn), np.diff(yn)
    slope = np.divide(dy, dx, out=np.zeros_like(dy), where=dx > 1e-12)
    positive = slope[slope > 1e-12]
    if positive.size == 0:
        return None
    n0 = max(1, len(slope) // 3)
    early = slope[:n0]
    early = early[early > 1e-12]
    ref = float(np.median(early)) if early.size else float(np.median(positive))
    threshold = 0.35 * ref
    chord = yn[0] + (yn[-1] - yn[0]) * xn
    dev = yn - chord
    center = int(np.nanargmax(dev[1:-1]) + 1) if len(dev) > 2 else len(x) // 2
    low_gain = center
    for i in range(max(1, center - 2), len(slope)):
        if slope[i] <= threshold:
            low_gain = i + 1
            break
    lo = max(0, min(center, low_gain) - 1)
    hi = min(len(x) - 1, max(center, low_gain) + 1)
    return {
        "x_low": float(x[lo]),
        "x_high": float(x[hi]),
        "x_center": float(x[center]),
        "y_center": float(y[center]),
    }


def _dash_regions(rep_x, labels, xlim):
    xs = np.asarray(rep_x, dtype=float)
    labs = np.asarray(labels, dtype=object)
    good = np.isfinite(xs)
    xs, labs = xs[good], labs[good]
    if len(xs) == 0:
        return []
    order = np.argsort(xs)
    xs, labs = xs[order], labs[order]
    bounds = [xlim[0]] + [(xs[i] + xs[i+1]) / 2 for i in range(len(xs)-1)] + [xlim[1]]
    return [(bounds[i], bounds[i+1], str(labs[i])) for i in range(len(xs))]


def _dash_axes(width, height, margin, xlim, ylim, xtitle, ytitle):
    left, top, right, bottom = margin
    x0, x1, y0, y1 = left, width-right, height-bottom, top
    p = [
        f'<line x1="{x0}" y1="{y0}" x2="{x1}" y2="{y0}" stroke="#7b8794"/>',
        f'<line x1="{x0}" y1="{y0}" x2="{x0}" y2="{y1}" stroke="#7b8794"/>'
    ]
    for i in range(6):
        t = i / 5
        xx = x0 + t * (x1-x0)
        yy = y0 - t * (y0-y1)
        xv = xlim[0] + t * (xlim[1]-xlim[0])
        yv = ylim[0] + t * (ylim[1]-ylim[0])
        p += [
            f'<line x1="{xx:.1f}" y1="{y0}" x2="{xx:.1f}" y2="{y1}" stroke="#edf1f4"/>',
            f'<text x="{xx:.1f}" y="{y0+18}" text-anchor="middle" font-size="11" fill="#6b7280">{xv:.2f}</text>',
            f'<line x1="{x0}" y1="{yy:.1f}" x2="{x1}" y2="{yy:.1f}" stroke="#edf1f4"/>',
            f'<text x="{x0-8}" y="{yy+4:.1f}" text-anchor="end" font-size="11" fill="#6b7280">{yv:.2f}</text>'
        ]
    p += [
        f'<text x="{(x0+x1)/2:.1f}" y="{height-8}" text-anchor="middle" font-size="12" fill="#374151">{_dash_esc(xtitle)}</text>',
        f'<text x="14" y="{(y0+y1)/2:.1f}" text-anchor="middle" font-size="12" fill="#374151" transform="rotate(-90 14 {(y0+y1)/2:.1f})">{_dash_esc(ytitle)}</text>'
    ]
    return p


def _dash_scatter(
    x, y, labels=None, xtitle="", ytitle="", small=None,
    regions=False, target=None, knee=None,
    groups=None, point_ids=None, point_radius=5.8,
):
    W, H = 1180, 680
    M = (90, 55, 35, 78)
    xs0, ys0 = np.asarray(x, float), np.asarray(y, float)
    good = np.isfinite(xs0) & np.isfinite(ys0)
    xs, ys = xs0[good], ys0[good]
    labs = np.asarray(labels, dtype=object)[good] if labels is not None else np.asarray([""]*len(xs), dtype=object)
    grps = np.asarray(groups, dtype=object)[good] if groups is not None else np.asarray(["TARGET"]*len(xs), dtype=object)
    ids = np.asarray(point_ids, dtype=object)[good] if point_ids is not None else np.asarray([""]*len(xs), dtype=object)

    ex, ey = [], []
    for a, b in (small or []):
        if np.isfinite(a) and np.isfinite(b):
            ex.append(float(a)); ey.append(float(b))
    ax = np.concatenate([xs, np.asarray(ex)]) if ex else xs
    ay = np.concatenate([ys, np.asarray(ey)]) if ey else ys
    if target is not None and np.isfinite(target):
        ay = np.concatenate([ay, [float(target)]])
    sx, xlim = _dash_scale(ax, M[0], W-M[2], .04)
    sy, ylim = _dash_scale(ay, H-M[3], M[1], .07)
    palette = dict(RISK_ECONOMIC_REGION_COLORS)

    p = [f'<svg viewBox="0 0 {W} {H}" width="100%">']
    if knee:
        a, b, c = sx(knee["x_low"]), sx(knee["x_high"]), sx(knee["x_center"])
        p += [
            f'<rect x="{min(a,b):.1f}" y="{M[1]}" width="{abs(b-a):.1f}" height="{H-M[1]-M[3]}" fill="#c0841a" opacity=".10"/>',
            f'<line x1="{c:.1f}" y1="{M[1]}" x2="{c:.1f}" y2="{H-M[3]}" stroke="#c0841a" stroke-width="2" stroke-dasharray="7 5"/>',
            f'<text x="{c+6:.1f}" y="{M[1]+38}" font-size="11" font-weight="700" fill="#a66d00">한계효과 둔화 참고구간</text>'
        ]
    p += _dash_axes(W, H, M, xlim, ylim, xtitle, ytitle)
    if target is not None and np.isfinite(target):
        yy = sy(target)
        p += [
            f'<line x1="{M[0]}" y1="{yy:.1f}" x2="{W-M[2]}" y2="{yy:.1f}" stroke="#a13d3d" stroke-width="1.7" stroke-dasharray="6 5"/>',
            f'<text x="{W-M[2]-4}" y="{yy-6:.1f}" text-anchor="end" font-size="11" fill="#a13d3d">Target Ao {target:.2f}%</text>'
        ]
    for a, b in zip(ex, ey):
