}


def _choose_quantile_rows(df: pd.DataFrame, n_max: int = 5) -> pd.DataFrame:
    if not isinstance(df, pd.DataFrame) or df.empty:
        return pd.DataFrame()
    q = df.copy()
    cols = [c for c in ["Stock_Units","NSGA_Cost_KRW","Probability_Any_Shortage","Alternative_ID"] if c in q.columns]
    if cols:
        q = q.sort_values(cols, kind="mergesort").reset_index(drop=True)
    n = len(q)
    take = min(int(n_max), n)
    if n <= take:
        return q
    idx = sorted(set(np.rint(np.linspace(0, n-1, take)).astype(int).tolist()))
    while len(idx) < take:
        rem = [i for i in range(n) if i not in idx]
        if not rem:
            break
        best = max(rem, key=lambda i: min(abs(i-j) for j in idx) if idx else n)
        idx.append(best)
        idx = sorted(idx)
    return q.iloc[idx[:take]].reset_index(drop=True)


def _apply_risk_economic_policy_regions(
    nsga_result: Dict[str, object],
    mc_result: Dict[str, object],
    decision_result: Dict[str, object],
    config: NSGAConfig,
) -> Tuple[Dict[str, object], Dict[str, object], Dict[str, object]]:
    """
    Final policy-region classification based on observed economic trade-off.

    Contract-target anchor:
      Lowest-cost actual representative satisfying Target Ao.
      If none satisfies Target Ao, closest-to-target Ao alternative is used.

    COST_SAVING:
      lower cost than anchor AND higher future shortage risk.

    STABILITY_ENHANCED:
      higher cost than anchor AND lower future shortage risk.

    TARGET:
      anchor itself and alternatives without either directional trade-off.

    Final policy regions therefore do not depend on arbitrary Ao +/- bands.
    """
    nsga = dict(nsga_result)
    mc = dict(mc_result)
    decision = dict(decision_result)

    reps = _ensure_nominal_ao_column(
        nsga.get("representative_alternatives_df", pd.DataFrame())
    )
    dt = decision.get("decision_table_df", pd.DataFrame()).copy()
    if reps.empty or dt.empty:
        return nsga, mc, decision

    # Preserve original Ao search-window labels for audit/reference.
    for frame in [reps, dt]:
        if "Ao_Band_ID" in frame.columns and "Search_Ao_Band_ID" not in frame.columns:
            frame["Search_Ao_Band_ID"] = frame["Ao_Band_ID"].astype(str)
        if "Ao_Band_Name" in frame.columns and "Search_Ao_Band_Name" not in frame.columns:
            frame["Search_Ao_Band_Name"] = frame["Ao_Band_Name"].astype(str)
        if "Strategy" in frame.columns and "Search_Strategy" not in frame.columns:
            frame["Search_Strategy"] = frame["Strategy"].astype(str)

    # Make decision table self-contained.
    rep_meta_cols = [c for c in [
        "Alternative_ID","Nominal_Ao","Worst_Ao","NSGA_Cost_KRW","Stock_Units"
    ] if c in reps.columns]
    rep_meta = reps[rep_meta_cols].drop_duplicates("Alternative_ID")
    add_cols = [c for c in rep_meta.columns if c != "Alternative_ID" and c not in dt.columns]
    if add_cols:
        dt = dt.merge(
            rep_meta[["Alternative_ID"] + add_cols],
            on="Alternative_ID", how="left", validate="one_to_one"
        )
    dt = _ensure_nominal_ao_column(dt)

    for c in ["NSGA_Cost_KRW","Probability_Any_Shortage","Nominal_Ao","Stock_Units"]:
        if c in dt.columns:
            dt[c] = pd.to_numeric(dt[c], errors="coerce")

    valid = dt.dropna(
        subset=["NSGA_Cost_KRW","Probability_Any_Shortage","Nominal_Ao"]
    ).copy()
    if valid.empty:
        return nsga, mc, decision

    target = float(config.target_ao)
    feasible = valid[valid["Nominal_Ao"] >= target - 1e-12].copy()
    if not feasible.empty:
        anchor = feasible.sort_values(
            ["NSGA_Cost_KRW","Probability_Any_Shortage","Stock_Units","Alternative_ID"],
            kind="mergesort",
        ).iloc[0]
        anchor_basis = "MIN_COST_TARGET_FEASIBLE"
    else:
        valid["__ao_gap"] = (valid["Nominal_Ao"] - target).abs()
        anchor = valid.sort_values(
            ["__ao_gap","NSGA_Cost_KRW","Probability_Any_Shortage","Alternative_ID"],
            kind="mergesort",
        ).iloc[0]
        anchor_basis = "CLOSEST_TO_TARGET_FALLBACK"

    anchor_id = str(anchor["Alternative_ID"])
    anchor_cost = float(anchor["NSGA_Cost_KRW"])
    anchor_risk = float(anchor["Probability_Any_Shortage"])
    anchor_ao = float(anchor["Nominal_Ao"])

    dt["Policy_Anchor_ID"] = anchor_id
    dt["Policy_Anchor_Basis"] = anchor_basis
    dt["Anchor_Cost_KRW"] = anchor_cost
    dt["Anchor_Risk"] = anchor_risk
    dt["Anchor_Ao"] = anchor_ao
    dt["Cost_Delta_vs_Anchor_KRW"] = dt["NSGA_Cost_KRW"] - anchor_cost
    dt["Risk_Delta_vs_Anchor"] = dt["Probability_Any_Shortage"] - anchor_risk
    dt["Ao_Delta_vs_Anchor"] = dt["Nominal_Ao"] - anchor_ao

    dt["Cost_Saving_vs_Anchor_KRW"] = np.maximum(-dt["Cost_Delta_vs_Anchor_KRW"], 0.0)
    dt["Risk_Increase_vs_Anchor"] = np.maximum(dt["Risk_Delta_vs_Anchor"], 0.0)
    dt["Additional_Cost_vs_Anchor_KRW"] = np.maximum(dt["Cost_Delta_vs_Anchor_KRW"], 0.0)
    dt["Risk_Reduction_vs_Anchor"] = np.maximum(-dt["Risk_Delta_vs_Anchor"], 0.0)

    saving_100m = dt["Cost_Saving_vs_Anchor_KRW"] / 1e8
    add_100m = dt["Additional_Cost_vs_Anchor_KRW"] / 1e8
    risk_inc_pp = dt["Risk_Increase_vs_Anchor"] * 100.0
    risk_red_pp = dt["Risk_Reduction_vs_Anchor"] * 100.0
    dt["Cost_Saving_per_Risk_PctPt_100M"] = np.where(
        risk_inc_pp > 1e-12, saving_100m / risk_inc_pp, np.nan
    )
    dt["Risk_Reduction_per_100M_vs_Anchor_PctPt"] = np.where(
        add_100m > 1e-12, risk_red_pp / add_100m, np.nan
    )

    cost_tol = max(1.0, abs(anchor_cost)*1e-9)
    risk_tol = 1e-9

    def _region(row):
        aid = str(row["Alternative_ID"])
        dc = float(row["Cost_Delta_vs_Anchor_KRW"])
        dr = float(row["Risk_Delta_vs_Anchor"])
        if aid == anchor_id:
            return "TARGET"
        if dc < -cost_tol and dr > risk_tol:
            return "COST_SAVING"
        if dc > cost_tol and dr < -risk_tol:
            return "STABILITY_ENHANCED"
        return "TARGET"

    dt["Ao_Band_ID"] = dt.apply(_region, axis=1)
    dt["Ao_Band_Name"] = dt["Ao_Band_ID"].map(RISK_ECONOMIC_REGION_NAMES)
    dt["Ao_Band_Order"] = dt["Ao_Band_ID"].map(AO_POLICY_BAND_ORDER).fillna(9).astype(int)

    def _desc(row):
        bid = str(row["Ao_Band_ID"])
        if bid == "COST_SAVING":
            return (
                f"기준 대비 {float(row['Cost_Saving_vs_Anchor_KRW'])/1e8:.2f}억원 절감 / "
                f"미래 부족위험 +{float(row['Risk_Increase_vs_Anchor'])*100:.2f}%p"
            )
        if bid == "STABILITY_ENHANCED":
            return (
                f"기준 대비 +{float(row['Additional_Cost_vs_Anchor_KRW'])/1e8:.2f}억원 / "
                f"미래 부족위험 -{float(row['Risk_Reduction_vs_Anchor'])*100:.2f}%p"
            )
        if str(row["Alternative_ID"]) == anchor_id:
            return "계약목표 기준대안"
        return "기준대안 주변 / 명확한 비용-Risk 방향성 없음"

    dt["Policy_Tradeoff_Description"] = dt.apply(_desc, axis=1)

    # Up to five already-simulated real alternatives per final region.
    map_rows = []
    for bid in ["COST_SAVING","TARGET","STABILITY_ENHANCED"]:
        sub = dt[dt["Ao_Band_ID"] == bid].copy()
        if sub.empty:
            continue
        chosen = _choose_quantile_rows(sub, 5)

        # Ensure anchor is retained in TARGET.
        if bid == "TARGET" and anchor_id in set(sub["Alternative_ID"].astype(str)):
            if anchor_id not in set(chosen["Alternative_ID"].astype(str)):
                ar = sub[sub["Alternative_ID"].astype(str) == anchor_id].iloc[[0]]
                chosen = pd.concat([chosen, ar], ignore_index=True)
                chosen = chosen.sort_values(
                    ["Stock_Units","NSGA_Cost_KRW","Alternative_ID"],
                    kind="mergesort",
                ).drop_duplicates("Alternative_ID")
                if len(chosen) > 5:
                    chosen = _choose_quantile_rows(chosen, 5)

        labels = _strategy_names_for_count(len(chosen))
        for i, (_, row) in enumerate(chosen.iterrows()):
            map_rows.append({
                "Alternative_ID": str(row["Alternative_ID"]),
                "Ao_Band_ID": bid,
                "Ao_Band_Name": RISK_ECONOMIC_REGION_NAMES[bid],
                "Ao_Band_Order": int(AO_POLICY_BAND_ORDER[bid]),
                "Strategy": labels[i],
            })

    map_df = pd.DataFrame(map_rows).drop_duplicates("Alternative_ID")
    if map_df.empty:
        decision["decision_table_df"] = dt
        nsga["representative_alternatives_df"] = reps
        return nsga, mc, decision

    selected_ids = map_df["Alternative_ID"].astype(str).tolist()

    # Final decision table.
    dt = dt[dt["Alternative_ID"].astype(str).isin(selected_ids)].copy()
    dt = dt.drop(columns=[c for c in ["Strategy","Ao_Band_ID","Ao_Band_Name","Ao_Band_Order"] if c in dt.columns])
    dt = dt.merge(map_df, on="Alternative_ID", how="left", validate="one_to_one")
    decision["decision_table_df"] = dt

    # Final representative table.
    reps = reps[reps["Alternative_ID"].astype(str).isin(selected_ids)].copy()
    reps = reps.drop(columns=[c for c in ["Strategy","Ao_Band_ID","Ao_Band_Name","Ao_Band_Order"] if c in reps.columns])
    reps = reps.merge(map_df, on="Alternative_ID", how="left", validate="one_to_one")
    metric_cols = [
        "Alternative_ID","Policy_Anchor_ID","Policy_Anchor_Basis",
        "Anchor_Cost_KRW","Anchor_Risk","Anchor_Ao",
        "Cost_Delta_vs_Anchor_KRW","Risk_Delta_vs_Anchor","Ao_Delta_vs_Anchor",
        "Cost_Saving_vs_Anchor_KRW","Risk_Increase_vs_Anchor",
        "Additional_Cost_vs_Anchor_KRW","Risk_Reduction_vs_Anchor",
        "Cost_Saving_per_Risk_PctPt_100M",
        "Risk_Reduction_per_100M_vs_Anchor_PctPt",
        "Policy_Tradeoff_Description",
    ]
    reps = reps.merge(
        dt[[c for c in metric_cols if c in dt.columns]].drop_duplicates("Alternative_ID"),
        on="Alternative_ID", how="left", validate="one_to_one"
    )
    nsga["representative_alternatives_df"] = reps

    # Apply same final identity to inventory, bridge and future outputs.
    def _filter_map(frame, validate):
        if not isinstance(frame, pd.DataFrame) or frame.empty or "Alternative_ID" not in frame.columns:
            return frame
        f = frame[frame["Alternative_ID"].astype(str).isin(selected_ids)].copy()
        for c in ["Strategy","Ao_Band_ID","Ao_Band_Name","Ao_Band_Order"]:
            if c in f.columns:
                f = f.drop(columns=[c])
        return f.merge(map_df, on="Alternative_ID", how="left", validate=validate)

    nsga["representative_inventory_df"] = _filter_map(
        nsga.get("representative_inventory_df", pd.DataFrame()), "many_to_one"
    )
    nsga["monte_carlo_bridge_df"] = _filter_map(
        nsga.get("monte_carlo_bridge_df", pd.DataFrame()), "many_to_one"
    )
    for key in ["mc_summary_df","mc_iteration_df","mc_part_summary_df"]:
        mc[key] = _filter_map(mc.get(key, pd.DataFrame()), "many_to_one")

    summary_rows = []
    for bid in ["COST_SAVING","TARGET","STABILITY_ENHANCED"]:
        sub = dt[dt["Ao_Band_ID"] == bid].copy()
        definition = {
            "COST_SAVING": "기준대안보다 비용이 낮고 미래 재고부족 Risk가 높음",
            "TARGET": "목표 Ao를 만족하는 최소비용 기준대안 및 중립 대안",
            "STABILITY_ENHANCED": "기준대안보다 비용이 높고 미래 재고부족 Risk가 낮음",
        }[bid]
        summary_rows.append({
            "Policy_Region_ID": bid,
            "Policy_Region_Name": RISK_ECONOMIC_REGION_NAMES[bid],
            "Definition": definition,
            "Anchor_Alternative_ID": anchor_id,
            "Anchor_Cost_KRW": anchor_cost,
            "Anchor_Risk": anchor_risk,
            "Anchor_Ao": anchor_ao,
            "Representative_Count": int(len(sub)),
        })

    nsga["policy_region_summary_df"] = pd.DataFrame(summary_rows)
    nsga["policy_anchor_df"] = pd.DataFrame([{
        "Anchor_Alternative_ID": anchor_id,
        "Anchor_Basis": anchor_basis,
        "Anchor_Cost_KRW": anchor_cost,
        "Anchor_Risk": anchor_risk,
        "Anchor_Ao": anchor_ao,
        "Target_Ao": target,
    }])
    return nsga, mc, decision


def _build_spare_detail_html(reps: pd.DataFrame, inventory: pd.DataFrame) -> str:
    """HTML tab panels for item-level spare quantities by selected strategy."""
    if (
        not isinstance(reps, pd.DataFrame) or reps.empty
        or not isinstance(inventory, pd.DataFrame) or inventory.empty
        or "Alternative_ID" not in reps.columns
        or "Alternative_ID" not in inventory.columns
    ):
        return '<div class="decision-note">수리부속 상세 산출내역 데이터가 없습니다.</div>'

    candidates = [
        ("part_id","Part ID"), ("maint_echelon","정비계층"),
        ("stocking_echelon","재고배치"), ("Stock_Qty","산출수량"),
        ("recommended_stock","산출수량"), ("stock_O","O"), ("stock_I","I"),
        ("stock_D","D"), ("unit_cost","단가(KRW)"),
        ("expected_demand_period","기간 기대수요"), ("p_need_period","수요발생확률"),
        ("lead_time","Lead Time(h)"), ("repairable_flag","수리가능"),
        ("repair_tat_h","Repair TAT(h)"), ("qpa","QPA"),
        ("priority_score","우선순위"),
    ]

    buttons, panels = [], []
    for i, (_, rr) in enumerate(reps.iterrows()):
        aid = str(rr["Alternative_ID"])
        region = str(rr.get("Ao_Band_Name",""))
        strategy = str(rr.get("Strategy",""))
        sub = inventory[inventory["Alternative_ID"].astype(str) == aid].copy()

        qty_col = next((c for c in ["Stock_Qty","recommended_stock","total_stock"] if c in sub.columns), None)
        if qty_col:
            sub[qty_col] = pd.to_numeric(sub[qty_col], errors="coerce").fillna(0)
            sub = sub[sub[qty_col] > 0].sort_values(qty_col, ascending=False)

        cols, rename, seen = [], {}, set()
        for c, label in candidates:
            if c in sub.columns and c not in seen:
                if c == "recommended_stock" and "Stock_Qty" in sub.columns:
                    continue
                cols.append(c); rename[c] = label; seen.add(c)

        table = (
            sub[cols].rename(columns=rename).to_html(
                index=False, classes="data-table spare-table", border=0, na_rep="-"
            )
            if cols else '<div class="decision-note">표시 가능한 상세 컬럼이 없습니다.</div>'
        )
        total_qty = int(pd.to_numeric(sub[qty_col], errors="coerce").fillna(0).sum()) if qty_col and not sub.empty else 0
        active = " active" if i == 0 else ""
        display = "block" if i == 0 else "none"

        buttons.append(
            f'<button class="spare-tab-btn{active}" data-alt="{_dash_esc(aid)}" '
            f'onclick="showSpareDetail(\'{_dash_esc(aid)}\')">'
            f'{_dash_esc(region)} · {_dash_esc(strategy)}</button>'
        )
        panels.append(
            f'<div class="spare-detail-panel" id="spare-detail-{_dash_esc(aid)}" style="display:{display}">'
            f'<div class="spare-detail-head"><div><h3>{_dash_esc(region)} · {_dash_esc(strategy)}</h3>'
            f'<p>{_dash_esc(aid)}</p></div><div class="spare-kpis">'
            f'<span>품목 {len(sub)}종</span><span>총 수량 {total_qty}EA</span></div></div>'
            f'<div class="table-card">{table}</div></div>'
        )
    return '<div class="spare-tabs">' + "".join(buttons) + '</div>' + "".join(panels)


def _build_step4_knee_analysis(
    nsga_result: Dict[str, object],
    decision_result: Dict[str, object],
    config: NSGAConfig,
) -> Dict[str, pd.DataFrame]:
    """
    Ao POLICY-BAND ARCHITECTURE · STEP 4

    Produce two DIFFERENT knee/saturation references:

    1) PRIMARY COST-Ao KNEE
       - Whole feasible NSGA alternative space
       - X = Cost
       - Y = achieved Ao
       - Meaning: where additional cost begins to produce materially smaller
         availability gains.

    2) SECONDARY WITHIN-BAND COST-RISK KNEE
       - One analysis per Ao policy band
       - X = Cost
       - Y = future shortage-risk reduction versus the lowest-stock
             representative inside that same band
       - Meaning: once the Ao policy level is held roughly constant, where
         additional cost begins to produce materially smaller risk reduction.

    These are descriptive decision references only.
    They never select or recommend an 'optimal' alternative.
    """
    all_runs = nsga_result.get("all_target_runs_df", pd.DataFrame()).copy()
    decision_df = decision_result.get("decision_table_df", pd.DataFrame()).copy()

    knee_rows: List[Dict[str, object]] = []
    marginal_rows: List[Dict[str, object]] = []

    # --------------------------------------------------------------
    # 4A. Primary whole-space Cost-Ao knee.
    # --------------------------------------------------------------
    if isinstance(all_runs, pd.DataFrame) and not all_runs.empty:
        ao_col = next(
            (c for c in ["Ao", "Nominal_Ao", "best_Ao", "Best_Ao"] if c in all_runs.columns),
            None,
        )
        if ao_col is not None and "total_cost" in all_runs.columns:
            q = all_runs.copy()
            q["__cost_100m"] = pd.to_numeric(
                q["total_cost"], errors="coerce"
            ) / 100_000_000.0
            q["__effect_ao_pct"] = (
                pd.to_numeric(q[ao_col], errors="coerce") * 100.0
            )

            if "Constraint_Violation" in q.columns:
                cv = pd.to_numeric(
                    q["Constraint_Violation"], errors="coerce"
                ).fillna(0.0)
                q = q[cv <= 1e-9].copy()

            q = (
                q.dropna(subset=["__cost_100m", "__effect_ao_pct"])
                .sort_values(["__cost_100m", "__effect_ao_pct"])
                .drop_duplicates(["__cost_100m", "__effect_ao_pct"])
            )

            k = _dash_knee_band(
                q["__cost_100m"].to_numpy(float),
                q["__effect_ao_pct"].to_numpy(float),
            )
            if k is not None:
                knee_rows.append({
                    "Knee_Type": "PRIMARY_COST_AO",
                    "Ao_Band_ID": "ALL",
                    "Ao_Band_Name": "전체 Cost-Ao 대안공간",
                    "Reference_Target_Ao": float(config.representative_target),
                    "X_Metric": "Cost_100M_KRW",
                    "Y_Metric": "Ao_Pct",
                    "Knee_X_Low": float(k["x_low"]),
                    "Knee_X_Center": float(k["x_center"]),
                    "Knee_X_High": float(k["x_high"]),
                    "Knee_Y_Center": float(k["y_center"]),
                    "Observed_Point_Count": int(len(q)),
                    "Interpretation": (
                        "비용 증가 대비 Ao 증가효과가 둔화되기 시작하는 "
                        "전체 대안공간의 참고구간"
                    ),
                    "Is_Optimization_Recommendation": False,
                })
            else:
                knee_rows.append({
                    "Knee_Type": "PRIMARY_COST_AO",
                    "Ao_Band_ID": "ALL",
                    "Ao_Band_Name": "전체 Cost-Ao 대안공간",
                    "Reference_Target_Ao": float(config.representative_target),
                    "X_Metric": "Cost_100M_KRW",
                    "Y_Metric": "Ao_Pct",
                    "Knee_X_Low": np.nan,
                    "Knee_X_Center": np.nan,
                    "Knee_X_High": np.nan,
                    "Knee_Y_Center": np.nan,
                    "Observed_Point_Count": int(len(q)),
                    "Interpretation": (
                        "안정적인 Cost-Ao Knee 참고구간 계산에 필요한 "
                        "유효 대안점이 충분하지 않음"
                    ),
                    "Is_Optimization_Recommendation": False,
                })

    # --------------------------------------------------------------
    # 4B. Secondary within-band Cost-Risk knees.
    # --------------------------------------------------------------
    if (
        isinstance(decision_df, pd.DataFrame)
        and not decision_df.empty
        and {
            "Alternative_ID",
            "Ao_Band_ID",
            "Ao_Band_Name",
            "Strategy",
            "Stock_Units",
            "NSGA_Cost_KRW",
            "Probability_Any_Shortage",
        }.issubset(decision_df.columns)
    ):
        work = decision_df.copy()
        work["NSGA_Cost_KRW"] = pd.to_numeric(
            work["NSGA_Cost_KRW"], errors="coerce"
        )
        work["Probability_Any_Shortage"] = pd.to_numeric(
            work["Probability_Any_Shortage"], errors="coerce"
        )
        work["Stock_Units"] = pd.to_numeric(
            work["Stock_Units"], errors="coerce"
        )

        band_order = ["COST_SAVING", "TARGET", "STABILITY_ENHANCED"]
        for band_id in band_order:
            b = work[work["Ao_Band_ID"].astype(str) == band_id].copy()
            b = b.dropna(
                subset=[
                    "NSGA_Cost_KRW",
                    "Probability_Any_Shortage",
                    "Stock_Units",
                ]
            )
            if b.empty:
                knee_rows.append({
                    "Knee_Type": "SECONDARY_COST_RISK",
                    "Ao_Band_ID": band_id,
                    "Ao_Band_Name": AO_POLICY_BAND_NAMES.get(
                        band_id, band_id
                    ),
                    "Reference_Target_Ao": float(config.representative_target),
                    "X_Metric": "Cost_100M_KRW",
                    "Y_Metric": "Risk_Reduction_PctPt",
                    "Knee_X_Low": np.nan,
                    "Knee_X_Center": np.nan,
                    "Knee_X_High": np.nan,
                    "Knee_Y_Center": np.nan,
                    "Observed_Point_Count": 0,
                    "Interpretation": "해당 Ao Band에 대표전략이 없음",
                    "Is_Optimization_Recommendation": False,
                })
                continue

            # Baseline = lowest-stock real representative within the same band.
            b = b.sort_values(
                ["Stock_Units", "NSGA_Cost_KRW", "Alternative_ID"],
                kind="mergesort",
            ).reset_index(drop=True)

            baseline_cost = float(b.iloc[0]["NSGA_Cost_KRW"])
            baseline_risk = float(
                b.iloc[0]["Probability_Any_Shortage"]
            )
            baseline_id = str(b.iloc[0]["Alternative_ID"])

            b["Cost_100M_KRW"] = (
                b["NSGA_Cost_KRW"] / 100_000_000.0
            )
            b["Additional_Cost_100M_KRW"] = (
                b["NSGA_Cost_KRW"] - baseline_cost
            ) / 100_000_000.0
            b["Risk_Reduction_PctPt"] = (
                baseline_risk - b["Probability_Any_Shortage"]
            ) * 100.0

            # Transition-level marginal effect.
            ordered = b.sort_values(
                ["NSGA_Cost_KRW", "Stock_Units", "Alternative_ID"],
                kind="mergesort",
            ).reset_index(drop=True)
            ordered["Prev_Alternative_ID"] = ordered[
                "Alternative_ID"
            ].shift(1)
            ordered["Prev_Strategy"] = ordered["Strategy"].shift(1)
            ordered["Delta_Cost_100M"] = (
                ordered["NSGA_Cost_KRW"] / 100_000_000.0
            ).diff()
            ordered["Delta_Risk_Reduction_PctPt"] = (
                ordered["Probability_Any_Shortage"].shift(1)
                - ordered["Probability_Any_Shortage"]
            ) * 100.0
            ordered["Marginal_Risk_Reduction_per_100M"] = np.where(
                ordered["Delta_Cost_100M"] > 1e-12,
                ordered["Delta_Risk_Reduction_PctPt"]
                / ordered["Delta_Cost_100M"],
                np.nan,
            )

            for i in range(1, len(ordered)):
                r = ordered.iloc[i]
                marginal_rows.append({
                    "Ao_Band_ID": band_id,
                    "Ao_Band_Name": str(
                        r.get(
                            "Ao_Band_Name",
                            AO_POLICY_BAND_NAMES.get(band_id, band_id),
                        )
                    ),
                    "From_Alternative_ID": str(
                        r.get("Prev_Alternative_ID", "")
                    ),
                    "To_Alternative_ID": str(
                        r.get("Alternative_ID", "")
                    ),
                    "From_Strategy": str(
                        r.get("Prev_Strategy", "")
                    ),
                    "To_Strategy": str(
                        r.get("Strategy", "")
                    ),
                    "Delta_Cost_100M_KRW": float(
                        r.get("Delta_Cost_100M", np.nan)
                    ),
                    "Delta_Risk_Reduction_PctPt": float(
                        r.get(
                            "Delta_Risk_Reduction_PctPt",
                            np.nan,
                        )
                    ),
                    "Marginal_Risk_Reduction_per_100M": float(
                        r.get(
                            "Marginal_Risk_Reduction_per_100M",
                            np.nan,
                        )
                    ),
                })

            # Cost vs cumulative risk reduction knee.
            k = _dash_knee_band(
                b["Cost_100M_KRW"].to_numpy(float),
                b["Risk_Reduction_PctPt"].to_numpy(float),
            )

            band_name = str(
                b.iloc[0].get(
                    "Ao_Band_Name",
                    AO_POLICY_BAND_NAMES.get(band_id, band_id),
                )
            )
            if k is not None:
                interpretation = (
                    "동일 Ao 정책수준 안에서 추가비용 대비 미래 "
                    "재고부족 위험감소의 한계효과가 둔화되기 시작하는 참고구간"
                )
                knee_rows.append({
                    "Knee_Type": "SECONDARY_COST_RISK",
                    "Ao_Band_ID": band_id,
                    "Ao_Band_Name": band_name,
                    "Reference_Target_Ao": float(config.representative_target),
                    "Band_MinStock_Baseline_ID": baseline_id,
                    "Baseline_Risk_Pct": baseline_risk * 100.0,
                    "X_Metric": "Cost_100M_KRW",
                    "Y_Metric": "Risk_Reduction_PctPt",
                    "Knee_X_Low": float(k["x_low"]),
                    "Knee_X_Center": float(k["x_center"]),
                    "Knee_X_High": float(k["x_high"]),
                    "Knee_Y_Center": float(k["y_center"]),
                    "Observed_Point_Count": int(len(b)),
                    "Interpretation": interpretation,
                    "Is_Optimization_Recommendation": False,
                })
            else:
                knee_rows.append({
                    "Knee_Type": "SECONDARY_COST_RISK",
                    "Ao_Band_ID": band_id,
                    "Ao_Band_Name": band_name,
                    "Reference_Target_Ao": float(config.representative_target),
                    "Band_MinStock_Baseline_ID": baseline_id,
                    "Baseline_Risk_Pct": baseline_risk * 100.0,
                    "X_Metric": "Cost_100M_KRW",
                    "Y_Metric": "Risk_Reduction_PctPt",
                    "Knee_X_Low": np.nan,
                    "Knee_X_Center": np.nan,
                    "Knee_X_High": np.nan,
                    "Knee_Y_Center": np.nan,
                    "Observed_Point_Count": int(len(b)),
                    "Interpretation": (
                        "해당 Band 내 안정적인 Cost-Risk Knee 계산에 "
                        "필요한 대표대안점이 충분하지 않음"
                    ),
                    "Is_Optimization_Recommendation": False,
                })

    knee_summary_df = pd.DataFrame(knee_rows)
    band_marginal_risk_df = pd.DataFrame(marginal_rows)

    return {
        "knee_summary_df": knee_summary_df,
        "band_marginal_risk_df": band_marginal_risk_df,
    }


def _build_step5_strategy_matrix(
    nsga_result: Dict[str, object],
    decision_result: Dict[str, object],
) -> Dict[str, pd.DataFrame]:
    """
    Ao POLICY-BAND ARCHITECTURE · STEP 5

    Build a transparent 3 x 5 strategy matrix.

    Rows:
      COST_SAVING / TARGET / STABILITY_ENHANCED

    Columns:
      최소재고형 / 경량 대응형 / 균형 대응형 / 안정형 / 최대안정형

    Empty cells remain empty. STEP 5 NEVER fabricates a strategy merely to
    complete the visual 3 x 5 grid.

    Outputs:
      - strategy_matrix_long_df : one record per expected matrix cell
      - strategy_matrix_view_df : human-readable 3 x 5 dashboard matrix
      - strategy_matrix_metric_df : normalized numeric data for later HTML
                                    rendering in STEP 6
    """
    reps = _ensure_nominal_ao_column(
        nsga_result.get(
            "representative_alternatives_df",
            pd.DataFrame(),
        )
    )
    decision = decision_result.get(
        "decision_table_df",
        pd.DataFrame(),
    ).copy()

    strategy_order = [
        "최소재고형",
        "경량 대응형",
        "균형 대응형",
        "안정형",
        "최대안정형",
    ]
    band_order = [
        ("COST_SAVING", AO_POLICY_BAND_NAMES["COST_SAVING"]),
        ("TARGET", AO_POLICY_BAND_NAMES["TARGET"]),
        (
            "STABILITY_ENHANCED",
            AO_POLICY_BAND_NAMES["STABILITY_ENHANCED"],
        ),
    ]

    # Merge engineering + future-risk decision data using unique ID.
    merged = reps.copy()
    if (
        isinstance(merged, pd.DataFrame)
        and not merged.empty
        and isinstance(decision, pd.DataFrame)
        and not decision.empty
        and "Alternative_ID" in merged.columns
        and "Alternative_ID" in decision.columns
    ):
        decision_keep = [
            c for c in [
                "Alternative_ID",
                "Probability_Any_Shortage",
                "Service_Mean",
                "Service_P05",
                "Shortage_P95",
                "Stability_Core",
                "Band_Additional_Cost_vs_MinStock_KRW",
                "Band_Risk_Reduction_vs_MinStock",
                "Band_Risk_Reduction_per_100M_KRW",
            ]
            if c in decision.columns
        ]
        merged = merged.merge(
            decision[decision_keep].drop_duplicates(
                "Alternative_ID"
            ),
            on="Alternative_ID",
            how="left",
            validate="one_to_one",
        )

    matrix_rows: List[Dict[str, object]] = []

    for band_id, band_name in band_order:
        for strategy_pos, strategy_name in enumerate(
            strategy_order,
            start=1,
        ):
            row = {
                "Ao_Band_ID": band_id,
                "Ao_Band_Name": band_name,
                "Ao_Band_Order": int(
                    AO_POLICY_BAND_ORDER.get(band_id, 99)
                ),
                "Strategy": strategy_name,
                "Strategy_Position": int(strategy_pos),
                "Cell_Status": "EMPTY",
                "Alternative_ID": "",
                "Nominal_Ao": np.nan,
                "Worst_Ao": np.nan,
                "NSGA_Cost_KRW": np.nan,
                "Stock_Units": np.nan,
                "Probability_Any_Shortage": np.nan,
                "Service_Mean": np.nan,
                "Service_P05": np.nan,
                "Shortage_P95": np.nan,
                "Stability_Core": np.nan,
                "Band_Additional_Cost_vs_MinStock_KRW": np.nan,
                "Band_Risk_Reduction_vs_MinStock": np.nan,
                "Band_Risk_Reduction_per_100M_KRW": np.nan,
            }

            if (
                isinstance(merged, pd.DataFrame)
                and not merged.empty
                and {
                    "Ao_Band_ID",
                    "Strategy",
                }.issubset(merged.columns)
            ):
                hit = merged[
                    (merged["Ao_Band_ID"].astype(str) == band_id)
                    & (
                        merged["Strategy"].astype(str)
                        == strategy_name
                    )
                ].copy()

                if not hit.empty:
                    # There should normally be one representative per cell.
                    # If duplicates occur, choose the stable lowest Alternative_ID
                    # and preserve transparency through Cell_Status.
                    hit = hit.sort_values(
                        ["Alternative_ID"],
                        kind="mergesort",
                    )
                    h = hit.iloc[0]
                    row["Cell_Status"] = (
                        "AVAILABLE"
                        if len(hit) == 1
                        else "MULTIPLE_SOURCE_ROWS"
                    )
                    for c in [
                        "Alternative_ID",
                        "Nominal_Ao",
                        "Worst_Ao",
                        "NSGA_Cost_KRW",
                        "Stock_Units",
                        "Probability_Any_Shortage",
                        "Service_Mean",
                        "Service_P05",
                        "Shortage_P95",
                        "Stability_Core",
                        "Band_Additional_Cost_vs_MinStock_KRW",
                        "Band_Risk_Reduction_vs_MinStock",
                        "Band_Risk_Reduction_per_100M_KRW",
                    ]:
                        if c in h.index:
                            row[c] = h[c]

                    for c in [
                        "Ao_Band_Low",
                        "Ao_Band_High",
                        "Ao_Band_Center",
                    ]:
                        if c in h.index:
                            row[c] = h[c]

            matrix_rows.append(row)

    matrix_long = pd.DataFrame(matrix_rows)

    # Human-readable cell text for the future dashboard.
    view = pd.DataFrame(
        index=[name for _, name in band_order],
        columns=strategy_order,
        dtype=object,
    )
    for _, r in matrix_long.iterrows():
        band_name = str(r["Ao_Band_Name"])
        strategy = str(r["Strategy"])
        if str(r["Cell_Status"]) == "EMPTY":
            view.loc[band_name, strategy] = "대안 없음"
            continue

        cost = pd.to_numeric(
            pd.Series([r.get("NSGA_Cost_KRW")]),
            errors="coerce",
        ).iloc[0]
        stock = pd.to_numeric(
            pd.Series([r.get("Stock_Units")]),
            errors="coerce",
        ).iloc[0]
        ao = pd.to_numeric(
            pd.Series([r.get("Nominal_Ao")]),
            errors="coerce",
        ).iloc[0]
        risk = pd.to_numeric(
            pd.Series([r.get("Probability_Any_Shortage")]),
            errors="coerce",
        ).iloc[0]

        parts = []
        if pd.notna(cost):
            parts.append(f"비용 {float(cost)/100_000_000.0:.2f}억")
        if pd.notna(stock):
            parts.append(f"재고 {int(round(float(stock)))}EA")
        if pd.notna(ao):
            parts.append(f"Ao {float(ao)*100.0:.2f}%")
        if pd.notna(risk):
            parts.append(f"부족위험 {float(risk)*100.0:.2f}%")
        view.loc[band_name, strategy] = " | ".join(parts)

    strategy_matrix_view_df = (
        view.reset_index()
        .rename(columns={"index": "Ao_Band_Name"})
    )

    metric_cols = [
        "Ao_Band_ID",
        "Ao_Band_Name",
        "Ao_Band_Order",
        "Strategy",
        "Strategy_Position",
        "Cell_Status",
        "Alternative_ID",
        "Ao_Band_Low",
        "Ao_Band_High",
        "Ao_Band_Center",
        "Nominal_Ao",
        "Worst_Ao",
        "NSGA_Cost_KRW",
        "Stock_Units",
        "Probability_Any_Shortage",
        "Service_Mean",
        "Service_P05",
        "Shortage_P95",
        "Stability_Core",
        "Band_Additional_Cost_vs_MinStock_KRW",
        "Band_Risk_Reduction_vs_MinStock",
        "Band_Risk_Reduction_per_100M_KRW",
    ]
    strategy_matrix_metric_df = matrix_long[
        [c for c in metric_cols if c in matrix_long.columns]
    ].copy()

    return {
        "strategy_matrix_long_df": matrix_long,
        "strategy_matrix_view_df": strategy_matrix_view_df,
        "strategy_matrix_metric_df": strategy_matrix_metric_df,
    }



def _ensure_nominal_ao_column(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """
    Normalize representative/decision tables to a stable Nominal_Ao column.

    Priority:
      1) Nominal_Ao
      2) Ao
      3) best_Ao / Best_Ao
      4) Worst_Ao (last-resort display fallback only)

    This prevents downstream HTML/Audit failures when an upstream table uses
    the native engine column name 'Ao' instead of 'Nominal_Ao'.
    """
    if not isinstance(df, pd.DataFrame):
        return pd.DataFrame()
    out = df.copy()

    if "Nominal_Ao" in out.columns:
        out["Nominal_Ao"] = pd.to_numeric(
            out["Nominal_Ao"], errors="coerce"
        )
        if out["Nominal_Ao"].notna().any():
            return out

    source = next(
        (
            c for c in ["Ao", "best_Ao", "Best_Ao", "Worst_Ao"]
            if c in out.columns
        ),
        None,
    )
    if source is None:
        out["Nominal_Ao"] = np.nan
    else:
        out["Nominal_Ao"] = pd.to_numeric(
            out[source], errors="coerce"
        )
    return out


def _detect_ao_column(df: pd.DataFrame) -> Optional[str]:
    """Return the best available Ao column without raising KeyError."""
    if not isinstance(df, pd.DataFrame):
        return None
    return next(
        (
            c for c in [
                "Nominal_Ao",
                "Ao",
                "best_Ao",
                "Best_Ao",
                "Worst_Ao",
            ]
            if c in df.columns
        ),
        None,
    )


# =============================================================================
# STEP 6 / STEP 7 FINAL INTEGRATION
# =============================================================================

def _step6_matrix_html(matrix_df: pd.DataFrame) -> str:
    """3×5 Strategy Matrix. Empty cells stay empty; no artificial alternatives."""
    if not isinstance(matrix_df, pd.DataFrame) or matrix_df.empty:
        return '<div class="decision-note">3×5 Strategy Matrix 데이터가 없습니다.</div>'

    bands = [
        ("COST_SAVING", "비용절감 Ao 영역"),
        ("TARGET", "계약목표 Ao 영역"),
        ("STABILITY_ENHANCED", "안정성강화 Ao 영역"),
    ]
    strategies = ["최소재고형", "경량 대응형", "균형 대응형", "안정형", "최대안정형"]

    body_rows = []
    for bid, bname in bands:
        tds = []
        for strategy in strategies:
            hit = matrix_df[
                (matrix_df["Ao_Band_ID"].astype(str) == bid)
                & (matrix_df["Strategy"].astype(str) == strategy)
            ]
            if hit.empty or str(hit.iloc[0].get("Cell_Status", "EMPTY")) == "EMPTY":
                tds.append(
                    '<td class="mx-empty">'
                    f'<div class="mx-title">{_dash_esc(strategy)}</div>'
                    '<div class="mx-none">대안 없음</div>'
                    '</td>'
                )
                continue

            r = hit.iloc[0]

            def n(col, scale=1.0):
                v = pd.to_numeric(pd.Series([r.get(col)]), errors="coerce").iloc[0]
                return None if pd.isna(v) else float(v) * scale

            cost = n("NSGA_Cost_KRW")
            stock = n("Stock_Units")
            ao = n("Nominal_Ao", 100.0)
            worst = n("Worst_Ao", 100.0)
            risk = n("Probability_Any_Shortage", 100.0)
            add_cost = n("Band_Additional_Cost_vs_MinStock_KRW")
            risk_red = n("Band_Risk_Reduction_vs_MinStock", 100.0)
            eff = n("Band_Risk_Reduction_per_100M_KRW")

            main = []
            if cost is not None:
                main.append(f"<b>{cost/1e8:.2f}억원</b>")
            if stock is not None:
                main.append(f"{int(round(stock))} EA")
            if ao is not None:
                main.append(f"Ao {ao:.2f}%")
            if risk is not None:
                main.append(f"부족위험 {risk:.2f}%")

            delta = ""
            if add_cost is not None and abs(add_cost) > 1e-9:
                delta = f'<div class="mx-delta">+{add_cost/1e8:.2f}억원'
                if risk_red is not None:
                    delta += f' / Risk -{risk_red:.2f}%p'
                delta += "</div>"

            small = []
            if worst is not None:
                small.append(f"Worst Ao {worst:.2f}%")
            if eff is not None and np.isfinite(eff):
                small.append(f"1억원당 Risk -{eff*100.0:.3f}%p")

            tds.append(
                '<td class="mx-cell">'
                f'<div class="mx-title">{_dash_esc(strategy)}</div>'
                f'<div class="mx-id">{_dash_esc(r.get("Alternative_ID",""))}</div>'
                f'<div class="mx-main">{" · ".join(main)}</div>'
                f'{delta}'
                + (f'<div class="mx-small">{" · ".join(small)}</div>' if small else "")
                + '</td>'
            )

        body_rows.append(
            '<tr>'
            f'<th class="mx-band">{_dash_esc(bname)}</th>'
            + "".join(tds)
            + '</tr>'
        )

    head = "".join(f"<th>{_dash_esc(s)}</th>" for s in strategies)
    return (
        '<div class="mx-wrap"><table class="mx-table">'
        f'<thead><tr><th>Ao 정책수준</th>{head}</tr></thead>'
        f'<tbody>{"".join(body_rows)}</tbody></table></div>'
    )


def _step6_band_panel(
    band_id: str,
    band_name: str,
    decision_df: pd.DataFrame,
    marginal_df: pd.DataFrame,
    knee_df: pd.DataFrame,
) -> str:
    """Within-band Cost/Risk and marginal-risk panel."""
    if (
        not isinstance(decision_df, pd.DataFrame)
        or decision_df.empty
        or "Ao_Band_ID" not in decision_df.columns
    ):
        return ""

    d = _ensure_nominal_ao_column(
        decision_df[
            decision_df["Ao_Band_ID"].astype(str) == band_id
        ].copy()
    )
    if d.empty:
        return (
            '<section class="band-panel">'
            f'<h3>{_dash_esc(band_name)}</h3>'
            '<div class="decision-note">'
            '현재 실제 NSGA 대안공간에는 이 Ao 정책영역의 대표전략이 없습니다. '
            '대시보드는 빈 영역을 임의의 전략으로 채우지 않습니다.'
            '</div></section>'
        )

    d = d.sort_values(
        ["Stock_Units", "NSGA_Cost_KRW", "Alternative_ID"],
        kind="mergesort",
    )
    labels = d["Strategy"].astype(str)
    cost = pd.to_numeric(d["NSGA_Cost_KRW"], errors="coerce") / 1e8
    risk = pd.to_numeric(d["Probability_Any_Shortage"], errors="coerce") * 100.0

    cost_risk = _dash_scatter(
        cost, risk, labels,
        xtitle="총 비용 (억원)",
        ytitle="미래 재고부족 발생확률 (%)",
        groups=pd.Series(band_id, index=d.index),
        point_ids=d.get("Alternative_ID", pd.Series("", index=d.index)),
        point_radius=5.8,
    )

    m = pd.DataFrame()
    if (
        isinstance(marginal_df, pd.DataFrame)
        and not marginal_df.empty
        and "Ao_Band_ID" in marginal_df.columns
    ):
        m = marginal_df[marginal_df["Ao_Band_ID"].astype(str) == band_id].copy()

    if not m.empty:
        trans = (
            m["From_Strategy"].astype(str)
            + " → "
            + m["To_Strategy"].astype(str)
        ).tolist()
        vals = pd.to_numeric(
            m["Marginal_Risk_Reduction_per_100M"], errors="coerce"
        ).fillna(0.0)
        marginal_svg = _dash_bar(
            trans, vals.tolist(), "1억원당 미래 Risk 감소 (%p/억원)"
        )
        mx = float(vals.max()) if len(vals) else np.nan
    else:
        marginal_svg = (
            '<div class="decision-note">'
            '전략 전환점이 충분하지 않아 한계 Risk 감소효과를 계산할 수 없습니다.'
            '</div>'
        )
        mx = np.nan

    knee_text = "Band 내부 Cost–Risk Knee 계산자료가 충분하지 않습니다."
    if (
        isinstance(knee_df, pd.DataFrame)
        and not knee_df.empty
        and {"Knee_Type", "Ao_Band_ID"}.issubset(knee_df.columns)
    ):
        k = knee_df[
            (knee_df["Knee_Type"].astype(str) == "SECONDARY_COST_RISK")
            & (knee_df["Ao_Band_ID"].astype(str) == band_id)
        ]
        if not k.empty:
            r = k.iloc[0]
            c = pd.to_numeric(pd.Series([r.get("Knee_X_Center")]), errors="coerce").iloc[0]
            lo = pd.to_numeric(pd.Series([r.get("Knee_X_Low")]), errors="coerce").iloc[0]
            hi = pd.to_numeric(pd.Series([r.get("Knee_X_High")]), errors="coerce").iloc[0]
            if pd.notna(c):
                knee_text = (
                    f"Band 내부 Cost–Risk 한계효과 둔화 참고구간은 "
                    f"약 {float(lo):.2f}~{float(hi):.2f}억원 "
                    f"(중심 {float(c):.2f}억원)입니다."
                )

    ao = pd.to_numeric(
        d.get("Nominal_Ao", pd.Series(np.nan, index=d.index)),
        errors="coerce",
    ) * 100.0
    tags = [
        f"Ao {ao.min():.2f}~{ao.max():.2f}%",
        f"비용 {cost.min():.2f}~{cost.max():.2f}억원",
        f"부족위험 {risk.min():.2f}~{risk.max():.2f}%",
    ]
    eff_text = f" 현재 관측 최대 한계효과는 1억원당 {mx:.3f}%p입니다." if np.isfinite(mx) else ""

    return (
        '<section class="band-panel">'
        '<div class="band-head">'
        f'<div><h3>{_dash_esc(band_name)}</h3>'
        '<p>같은 Ao 정책수준 안에서 비용과 미래 위험의 대응강도를 비교합니다.</p></div>'
        f'<div class="band-tags">{"".join(f"<span>{_dash_esc(x)}</span>" for x in tags)}</div>'
        '</div>'
        '<div class="decision-note"><b>Band 내부 해석</b><br>'
        f'{_dash_esc(knee_text)} '
        '이 위치는 특정 전략의 자동 선택점이 아니라 동일 성능수준에서 '
        '추가비용 대비 미래 위험감소의 성격이 달라지는 참고영역입니다.'
        f'{_dash_esc(eff_text)}</div>'
        '<div class="two-col">'
        '<div class="chart-card"><div class="chart-title">비용 ↔ 미래 재고부족 위험</div>'
        '<div class="chart-subtitle">같은 Ao 범위에서 비용 증가가 미래 부족확률을 얼마나 낮추는지 봅니다.</div>'
        f'{cost_risk}</div>'
        '<div class="chart-card"><div class="chart-title">추가 1억원당 미래 Risk 감소효과</div>'
        '<div class="chart-subtitle">전략을 한 단계 상향할 때 추가비용의 한계효과가 어디서 둔화되는지 봅니다.</div>'
        f'{marginal_svg}</div>'
        '</div></section>'
    )


def _augment_step7_final_audit(
    audit_result: Dict[str, object],
    nsga_result: Dict[str, object],
    mc_result: Dict[str, object],
    decision_result: Dict[str, object],
    config: NSGAConfig,
) -> Dict[str, object]:
    """Extend final audit to the completed STEP1~7 architecture."""
    out = dict(audit_result)
    base = out.get("audit_checks_df", pd.DataFrame()).copy()
    rows = []

    def add(name, ok, detail):
        rows.append({
            "Check": name,
            "Status": "PASS" if bool(ok) else "FAIL",
            "Severity": "ERROR",
            "Detail": str(detail),
        })

    expected_bands = ["COST_SAVING", "TARGET", "STABILITY_ENHANCED"]

    band_cfg = nsga_result.get("ao_policy_band_config_df", pd.DataFrame()).copy()
    ids_ok = False
    nonoverlap_ok = False
    ids = []
    if isinstance(band_cfg, pd.DataFrame) and not band_cfg.empty:
        ids = band_cfg["Ao_Band_ID"].astype(str).tolist()
        ids_ok = ids == expected_bands
        lo = pd.to_numeric(band_cfg["Ao_Band_Low"], errors="coerce").to_numpy(float)
        hi = pd.to_numeric(band_cfg["Ao_Band_High"], errors="coerce").to_numpy(float)
        nonoverlap_ok = (
            len(lo) == 3
            and np.isfinite(lo).all()
            and np.isfinite(hi).all()
            and (lo <= hi).all()
            and hi[0] < lo[1]
            and hi[1] < lo[2]
        )
    add("step7_search_window_ids", ids_ok, f"internal search windows expected={expected_bands}, actual={ids}")
    add("step7_search_window_nonoverlap", nonoverlap_ok, "internal Ao search windows ordered and non-overlapping")

    reps = _ensure_nominal_ao_column(
        nsga_result.get("representative_alternatives_df", pd.DataFrame())
    )
    rep_unique = False
    max5 = False
    policy_direction_ok = False
    counts = {}
    if isinstance(reps, pd.DataFrame) and not reps.empty:
        rep_unique = "Alternative_ID" in reps.columns and reps["Alternative_ID"].astype(str).is_unique
        counts = reps.groupby("Ao_Band_ID").size().to_dict() if "Ao_Band_ID" in reps.columns else {}
        max5 = all(int(v) <= 5 for v in counts.values()) and set(counts).issubset(set(expected_bands))
        req = {"Ao_Band_ID","Cost_Delta_vs_Anchor_KRW","Risk_Delta_vs_Anchor","Policy_Anchor_ID"}
        if req.issubset(reps.columns):
            checks = []
            for _, r in reps.iterrows():
                bid = str(r["Ao_Band_ID"])
                dc = float(r["Cost_Delta_vs_Anchor_KRW"])
                dr = float(r["Risk_Delta_vs_Anchor"])
                aid = str(r["Alternative_ID"])
                anchor_id = str(r["Policy_Anchor_ID"])
                if bid == "COST_SAVING":
                    checks.append(dc < 0 and dr > 0)
                elif bid == "STABILITY_ENHANCED":
                    checks.append(dc > 0 and dr < 0)
                elif bid == "TARGET":
                    checks.append(True if aid == anchor_id else not (dc < 0 and dr > 0) and not (dc > 0 and dr < 0))
                else:
                    checks.append(False)
            policy_direction_ok = bool(all(checks)) if checks else False
    add("step7_representative_id_unique", rep_unique, f"representatives={len(reps)}")
    add("step7_max_five_representatives_per_band", max5, f"counts={counts}")
    add(
        "step7_cost_risk_policy_direction",
        policy_direction_ok,
        "COST_SAVING=lower cost+higher risk; STABILITY_ENHANCED=higher cost+lower risk versus contract anchor",
    )

    future = mc_result.get("mc_summary_df", pd.DataFrame()).copy()
    future_map = False
    future_band = False
    rep_ids, future_ids = set(), set()
    if (
        isinstance(reps, pd.DataFrame) and not reps.empty
        and isinstance(future, pd.DataFrame) and not future.empty
        and "Alternative_ID" in reps.columns and "Alternative_ID" in future.columns
    ):
        rep_ids = set(reps["Alternative_ID"].astype(str))
        future_ids = set(future["Alternative_ID"].astype(str))
        future_map = rep_ids == future_ids
        if "Ao_Band_ID" in reps.columns and "Ao_Band_ID" in future.columns:
            mp = (
                reps[["Alternative_ID", "Ao_Band_ID"]]
                .drop_duplicates("Alternative_ID")
                .set_index("Alternative_ID")["Ao_Band_ID"].astype(str).to_dict()
            )
            expected = future["Alternative_ID"].astype(str).map(mp)
            future_band = bool((future["Ao_Band_ID"].astype(str) == expected.astype(str)).all())
    add("step7_future_alternative_mapping", future_map, f"rep={len(rep_ids)}, future={len(future_ids)}")
    add("step7_future_band_identity", future_band, "future output retains correct Ao_Band_ID")

    matrix = nsga_result.get("strategy_matrix_long_df", pd.DataFrame()).copy()
    matrix_shape = False
    matrix_link = False
    if isinstance(matrix, pd.DataFrame) and not matrix.empty:
        matrix_shape = (
            len(matrix) == 15
            and set(matrix["Ao_Band_ID"].astype(str)) == set(expected_bands)
            and matrix.groupby("Ao_Band_ID").size().eq(5).all()
        )
        available = matrix[matrix["Cell_Status"].astype(str) != "EMPTY"]
        mids = set(available["Alternative_ID"].astype(str))
        mids.discard("")
        rids = set(reps["Alternative_ID"].astype(str)) if not reps.empty else set()
        matrix_link = mids == rids
        mdetail = f"cells={len(matrix)}, available={len(available)}, representatives={len(rids)}"
    else:
        mdetail = "matrix missing"
    add("step7_strategy_matrix_shape_3x5", matrix_shape, mdetail)
    add("step7_strategy_matrix_real_rep_link", matrix_link, "available cells map exactly to real representatives")

    knee = nsga_result.get("knee_summary_df", pd.DataFrame()).copy()
    primary = False
    secondary = False
    no_auto = False
    if isinstance(knee, pd.DataFrame) and not knee.empty:
        kt = knee["Knee_Type"].astype(str)
        primary = int((kt == "PRIMARY_COST_AO").sum()) == 1
        populated = set(reps["Ao_Band_ID"].astype(str)) if not reps.empty else set()
        secbands = set(knee.loc[kt == "SECONDARY_COST_RISK", "Ao_Band_ID"].astype(str))
        secondary = populated.issubset(secbands)
        if "Is_Optimization_Recommendation" in knee.columns:
            no_auto = bool((~knee["Is_Optimization_Recommendation"].fillna(False).astype(bool)).all())
    add("step7_primary_cost_ao_knee", primary, "one PRIMARY_COST_AO record")
    add("step7_secondary_cost_risk_knee", secondary, "each populated band has SECONDARY_COST_RISK")
    add("step7_knee_not_auto_recommendation", no_auto, "knee remains descriptive reference only")

    marginal = decision_result.get("band_marginal_risk_df", pd.DataFrame()).copy()
    marginal_ok = True
    n_checked = 0
    if isinstance(marginal, pd.DataFrame) and not marginal.empty:
        dc = pd.to_numeric(marginal["Delta_Cost_100M_KRW"], errors="coerce")
        dr = pd.to_numeric(marginal["Delta_Risk_Reduction_PctPt"], errors="coerce")
        act = pd.to_numeric(marginal["Marginal_Risk_Reduction_per_100M"], errors="coerce")
        mask = dc > 1e-12
        marginal_ok = bool(np.allclose(
            (dr[mask] / dc[mask]).to_numpy(float),
            act[mask].to_numpy(float),
            atol=1e-10, rtol=1e-10, equal_nan=True,
        ))
        n_checked = int(mask.sum())
    add("step7_marginal_risk_formula", marginal_ok, f"checked={n_checked}")

    cand = nsga_result.get("candidates_df", pd.DataFrame()).copy()
    bound_meta = False
    bound_positive = False
    legacy_retired = False
    if isinstance(cand, pd.DataFrame) and not cand.empty:
        req = {
            "stock_search_upper_bound",
            "stock_search_bound_method",
            "stock_search_bound_quantile",
            "stock_search_bound_kmax",
        }
        bound_meta = req.issubset(cand.columns)
        if bound_meta:
            ub = pd.to_numeric(cand["stock_search_upper_bound"], errors="coerce")
            bound_positive = bool(ub.notna().all() and (ub >= 1).all())
            legacy_retired = bool(
                (cand["stock_search_bound_method"].astype(str) == "POISSON_QUANTILE_ROBUST_PIPELINE").all()
            )
    add("step7_dynamic_stock_bound_metadata", bound_meta, "probabilistic search-bound metadata available")
    add("step7_dynamic_stock_bound_positive", bound_positive, "all candidate search upper bounds >= 1")
    add("step7_legacy_caps_retired", legacy_retired, "3/4/6 and 999 are not active business caps")
    add(
        "step7_technical_guard_default_off",
        not bool(getattr(config, "technical_stock_guard_enabled", False)),
        f"technical_stock_guard_enabled={getattr(config,'technical_stock_guard_enabled',None)}",
    )

    inv = nsga_result.get("representative_inventory_df", pd.DataFrame()).copy()
    stock_domain = False
    if (
        isinstance(inv, pd.DataFrame) and not inv.empty
        and isinstance(cand, pd.DataFrame) and not cand.empty
        and {"part_id", "Stock_Qty"}.issubset(inv.columns)
        and {"part_id", "stock_search_upper_bound"}.issubset(cand.columns)
    ):
        mp = (
            cand[["part_id", "stock_search_upper_bound"]]
            .drop_duplicates("part_id")
            .set_index("part_id")["stock_search_upper_bound"]
        )
        chk = inv[["part_id", "Stock_Qty"]].copy()
        chk["upper"] = chk["part_id"].map(mp)
        stock = pd.to_numeric(chk["Stock_Qty"], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
        upper = pd.to_numeric(chk["upper"], errors="coerce")
        have = upper.notna()
        stock_domain = bool(
            (stock[have] <= upper[have] + 1e-12).all()
            and (stock[~have] <= 1e-12).all()
        )
    add("step7_selected_stock_within_dynamic_domain", stock_domain, "selected stock never exceeds item-specific dynamic search domain")

    step7_df = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    checks = pd.concat([base, step7_df], ignore_index=True) if isinstance(base, pd.DataFrame) and not base.empty else step7_df

    total = int(len(checks))
    passed = int((checks["Status"] == "PASS").sum())
    failed = total - passed
    overall = "PASS" if failed == 0 else "CHECK"

    out["audit_checks_df"] = checks
    out["audit_step7_checks_df"] = step7_df
    out["audit_summary_df"] = pd.DataFrame([{
        "Overall_Status": overall,
        "Total_Checks": total,
        "Pass_Count": passed,
        "Fail_Count": failed,
        "Architecture": "STEP1_TO_STEP7_COMPLETE",
        "Representative_Alternatives": int(len(reps)),
        "Strategy_Matrix_Cells": int(len(matrix)) if isinstance(matrix, pd.DataFrame) else 0,
        "Dynamic_Stock_Bound_Audited": True,
        "Automatic_Best_Selection": False,
    }])
    out["audit_summary"] = {
        "overall_status": overall,
        "total_checks": total,
        "pass_count": passed,
        "fail_count": failed,
    }
    return out



def _risk_change_level_text(delta_pp: float) -> str:
    """Directional text only; avoids arbitrary certification-style grades."""
    if not np.isfinite(delta_pp) or abs(delta_pp) < 1e-10:
        return "기준"
    return "Risk 감소" if delta_pp < 0 else "Risk 증가"


def _build_risk_energy_grade_html(decision_df: pd.DataFrame) -> str:
    """
    Energy-label-inspired Risk change view.

    The contract-target anchor is fixed at 0. Each strategy is shown by the
    ACTUAL shortage-risk difference versus the anchor:
      negative = risk reduction
      zero     = anchor
      positive = risk increase

    Bar length is normalized only for visual width; the exact %p is always
    printed, so no arbitrary risk grade threshold is introduced.
    """
    if (
        not isinstance(decision_df, pd.DataFrame)
        or decision_df.empty
        or "Risk_Delta_vs_Anchor" not in decision_df.columns
    ):
        return '<div class="decision-note">Risk 변화 등급을 표시할 데이터가 없습니다.</div>'

    d = decision_df.copy()
    d["Risk_Delta_PctPt"] = (
        pd.to_numeric(d["Risk_Delta_vs_Anchor"], errors="coerce") * 100.0
    )
    d["Cost_Delta_100M"] = (
        pd.to_numeric(d.get("Cost_Delta_vs_Anchor_KRW"), errors="coerce") / 1e8
    )
    d["Current_Risk_Pct"] = (
        pd.to_numeric(d.get("Probability_Any_Shortage"), errors="coerce") * 100.0
    )
    d = d.dropna(subset=["Risk_Delta_PctPt"]).copy()
    if d.empty:
        return '<div class="decision-note">Risk 변화 등급을 표시할 유효 데이터가 없습니다.</div>'

    # Most risk-reducing strategies first; most risk-increasing strategies last.
    d = d.sort_values(
        ["Risk_Delta_PctPt", "NSGA_Cost_KRW", "Alternative_ID"],
        kind="mergesort",
    ).reset_index(drop=True)

    max_abs = max(float(d["Risk_Delta_PctPt"].abs().max()), 0.01)

    rows = []
    for _, r in d.iterrows():
        delta = float(r["Risk_Delta_PctPt"])
        cost_delta = float(r["Cost_Delta_100M"]) if pd.notna(r["Cost_Delta_100M"]) else np.nan
        current_risk = float(r["Current_Risk_Pct"]) if pd.notna(r["Current_Risk_Pct"]) else np.nan
        aid = str(r.get("Alternative_ID", ""))
        strategy = str(r.get("Strategy", ""))
        region = str(r.get("Ao_Band_Name", ""))

        # Visual width only. Exact percentage-point delta is printed separately.
        width = 9.0 if abs(delta) < 1e-10 else max(18.0, 96.0 * abs(delta) / max_abs)

        if delta < -1e-10:
            cls = "risk-down"
            arrow = "◀"
            delta_text = f"{delta:.2f}%p"
            direction = "Risk 감소"
        elif delta > 1e-10:
            cls = "risk-up"
            arrow = "▶"
            delta_text = f"+{delta:.2f}%p"
            direction = "Risk 증가"
        else:
            cls = "risk-base"
            arrow = "◆"
            delta_text = "0.00%p"
            direction = "기준"

        if np.isfinite(cost_delta):
            if cost_delta < -1e-10:
                cost_text = f"비용 {abs(cost_delta):.2f}억원 절감"
            elif cost_delta > 1e-10:
                cost_text = f"비용 +{cost_delta:.2f}억원"
            else:
                cost_text = "기준 비용"
        else:
            cost_text = "-"

        risk_text = f"현재 부족위험 {current_risk:.2f}%" if np.isfinite(current_risk) else ""

        rows.append(
            '<div class="risk-grade-row">'
            '<div class="risk-grade-label">'
            f'<b>{_dash_esc(strategy)}</b>'
            f'<span>{_dash_esc(region)}</span>'
            '</div>'
            '<div class="risk-grade-track">'
            '<div class="risk-center-line"></div>'
            + (
                f'<div class="risk-grade-bar left {cls}" style="width:{width:.1f}%;" '
                f'onclick="showSpareDetail(\'{_dash_esc(aid)}\')">'
                f'<span>{arrow} {_dash_esc(direction)} {delta_text}</span></div>'
                if delta < -1e-10
                else (
                    f'<div class="risk-grade-bar right {cls}" style="width:{width:.1f}%;" '
                    f'onclick="showSpareDetail(\'{_dash_esc(aid)}\')">'
                    f'<span>{_dash_esc(direction)} {delta_text} {arrow}</span></div>'
                    if delta > 1e-10
                    else
                    f'<div class="risk-grade-anchor {cls}" '
                    f'onclick="showSpareDetail(\'{_dash_esc(aid)}\')">'
                    f'<span>{arrow} 기준 0.00%p</span></div>'
                )
            )
            + '</div>'
            '<div class="risk-grade-metrics">'
            f'<b>{delta_text}</b>'
            f'<span>{_dash_esc(cost_text)}</span>'
            f'<span>{_dash_esc(risk_text)}</span>'
            '</div>'
            '</div>'
        )

    return (
        '<div class="risk-grade-scale">'
        '<div class="risk-scale-left">Risk 감소 ◀</div>'
        '<div class="risk-scale-center">기준대안 0%p</div>'
        '<div class="risk-scale-right">▶ Risk 증가</div>'
        '</div>'
        '<div class="risk-grade-list">'
        + "".join(rows)
        + '</div>'
        '<div class="risk-grade-foot">'
        '막대 길이는 현재 대표전략 중 기준대안 대비 Risk 변화폭의 상대적 크기를 보여주며, '
        '정확한 판단값은 각 행의 %p 수치를 사용합니다.'
        '</div>'
    )


def _dash_cost_delta_vs_risk_delta(decision_df: pd.DataFrame) -> str:
    """Anchor-centered Cost change vs Risk change scatter."""
    if (
        not isinstance(decision_df, pd.DataFrame)
        or decision_df.empty
        or not {
            "Cost_Delta_vs_Anchor_KRW",
            "Risk_Delta_vs_Anchor",
            "Alternative_ID",
            "Strategy",
        }.issubset(decision_df.columns)
    ):
        return '<div class="decision-note">비용–Risk 증감 그래프 데이터가 없습니다.</div>'

    W, H = 1180, 650
    M = (90, 55, 35, 80)

    d = decision_df.copy()
    x = pd.to_numeric(d["Cost_Delta_vs_Anchor_KRW"], errors="coerce") / 1e8
    y = pd.to_numeric(d["Risk_Delta_vs_Anchor"], errors="coerce") * 100.0
    good = np.isfinite(x.to_numpy(float)) & np.isfinite(y.to_numpy(float))
    d = d.loc[good].copy()
    xs = x.loc[good].to_numpy(float)
    ys = y.loc[good].to_numpy(float)
    if len(xs) == 0:
        return '<div class="decision-note">비용–Risk 증감 그래프의 유효 데이터가 없습니다.</div>'

    # Always include zero axes.
    sx, xlim = _dash_scale(np.concatenate([xs, [0.0]]), M[0], W-M[2], .08)
    sy, ylim = _dash_scale(np.concatenate([ys, [0.0]]), H-M[3], M[1], .10)

    p = [f'<svg viewBox="0 0 {W} {H}" width="100%">']
    p += _dash_axes(
        W, H, M, xlim, ylim,
        "기준대비 비용 증감 (억원)",
        "기준대비 미래 부족위험 증감 (%p)",
    )

    # Quadrant guides.
    x0, y0 = sx(0.0), sy(0.0)
    p += [
        f'<line x1="{x0:.1f}" y1="{M[1]}" x2="{x0:.1f}" y2="{H-M[3]}" stroke="#7b8794" stroke-width="1.4" stroke-dasharray="5 5"/>',
        f'<line x1="{M[0]}" y1="{y0:.1f}" x2="{W-M[2]}" y2="{y0:.1f}" stroke="#7b8794" stroke-width="1.4" stroke-dasharray="5 5"/>',
        f'<text x="{M[0]+10}" y="{M[1]+18}" font-size="11" fill="#2f7d4a">비용절감 + Risk감소</text>',
        f'<text x="{W-M[2]-10}" y="{M[1]+18}" text-anchor="end" font-size="11" fill="#2f7d4a">추가비용 + Risk감소</text>',
        f'<text x="{M[0]+10}" y="{H-M[3]-10}" font-size="11" fill="#9b4b4b">비용절감 + Risk증가</text>',
        f'<text x="{W-M[2]-10}" y="{H-M[3]-10}" text-anchor="end" font-size="11" fill="#9b4b4b">추가비용 + Risk증가</text>',
    ]

    for (_, r), a, b in zip(d.iterrows(), xs, ys):
        aid = str(r.get("Alternative_ID", ""))
        strategy = str(r.get("Strategy", ""))
        if b < -1e-10:
            color = "#2F7D4A"
        elif b > 1e-10:
            color = "#B54B4B"
        else:
            color = "#7B8794"

        p.append(
            f'<circle cx="{sx(a):.1f}" cy="{sy(b):.1f}" r="6.0" '
            f'fill="{color}" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="1.3" class="strategy-point" '
            f'onclick="showSpareDetail(\'{_dash_esc(aid)}\')">'
            f'<title>{_dash_esc(strategy)} | 비용 {a:+.2f}억원 | Risk {b:+.2f}%p</title></circle>'
        )
        p.append(
            f'<text x="{sx(a):.1f}" y="{sy(b)-10:.1f}" text-anchor="middle" '
            f'font-size="10.5" fill="#172033">{_dash_esc(strategy)}</text>'
        )

    p.append('</svg>')
    return ''.join(p)


def _export_dashboard_html(
    output_html,
    nsga_result,
    mc_result,
    decision_result,
    audit_result,
    cfg,
    mc_cfg,
    decision_cfg,
):
    """STEP6 final offline dashboard."""
    out = _Path(output_html)

    reps = _ensure_nominal_ao_column(
        nsga_result.get("representative_alternatives_df", pd.DataFrame())
    )
    matrix = nsga_result.get("strategy_matrix_long_df", pd.DataFrame()).copy()
    knee = nsga_result.get("knee_summary_df", pd.DataFrame()).copy()
    marginal = decision_result.get("band_marginal_risk_df", pd.DataFrame()).copy()
    decision = decision_result.get("decision_table_df", pd.DataFrame()).copy()
    future = mc_result.get("mc_summary_df", pd.DataFrame()).copy()
    audit_all = audit_result.get("audit_checks_df", pd.DataFrame()).copy()
    audit7 = audit_result.get("audit_step7_checks_df", pd.DataFrame()).copy()
    inventory = nsga_result.get("representative_inventory_df", pd.DataFrame()).copy()
    policy_summary = nsga_result.get("policy_region_summary_df", pd.DataFrame()).copy()
    policy_anchor = nsga_result.get("policy_anchor_df", pd.DataFrame()).copy()

    # Whole-space Cost-Ao.
    ce_cloud, ce_ao_col = _dash_cost_ao_cloud(nsga_result)
    cloud = []
    if ce_ao_col is not None and not ce_cloud.empty:
        cloud = list(zip(
            (pd.to_numeric(ce_cloud["total_cost"], errors="coerce") / 1e8).tolist(),
            (pd.to_numeric(ce_cloud[ce_ao_col], errors="coerce") * 100.0).tolist(),
        ))

    pk = None
    if isinstance(knee, pd.DataFrame) and not knee.empty:
        qk = knee[knee["Knee_Type"].astype(str) == "PRIMARY_COST_AO"]
        if not qk.empty:
            r = qk.iloc[0]
            c = pd.to_numeric(pd.Series([r.get("Knee_X_Center")]), errors="coerce").iloc[0]
            if pd.notna(c):
                pk = {
                    "x_low": float(r.get("Knee_X_Low")),
                    "x_center": float(c),
                    "x_high": float(r.get("Knee_X_High")),
                    "y_center": float(r.get("Knee_Y_Center")),
                }

    top_svg = ""
    if isinstance(reps, pd.DataFrame) and not reps.empty:
        labels = reps["Ao_Band_Name"].astype(str) + " / " + reps["Strategy"].astype(str)
        top_svg = _dash_scatter(
            pd.to_numeric(reps["NSGA_Cost_KRW"], errors="coerce") / 1e8,
            pd.to_numeric(
                reps.get("Nominal_Ao", pd.Series(np.nan, index=reps.index)),
                errors="coerce",
            ) * 100.0,
            labels,
            xtitle="총 비용 (억원)",
            ytitle="운용가용도 Ao (%)",
            small=cloud,
            regions=False,
            target=float(cfg.target_ao) * 100.0,
            knee=pk,
            groups=reps.get("Ao_Band_ID", pd.Series("TARGET", index=reps.index)),
            point_ids=reps.get("Alternative_ID", pd.Series("", index=reps.index)),
            point_radius=5.8,
        )

    # Final cost/risk policy-region cards.
    band_strip = ""
    if isinstance(policy_summary, pd.DataFrame) and not policy_summary.empty:
        cards = []
        for _, r in policy_summary.iterrows():
            rid = str(r.get("Policy_Region_ID",""))
            color = RISK_ECONOMIC_REGION_COLORS.get(rid, "#7B8794")
            cards.append(
                '<div class="band-card" style="border-top:4px solid '+color+'">'
                f'<b>{_dash_esc(r.get("Policy_Region_Name",""))}</b>'
                f'<span>{_dash_esc(r.get("Definition",""))}</span>'
                f'<span>대표전략 {int(r.get("Representative_Count",0))}개</span>'
                '</div>'
            )
        band_strip = '<div class="band-strip">' + "".join(cards) + '</div>'

    matrix_html = _step6_matrix_html(matrix)

    band_panels = "".join([
        _step6_band_panel("COST_SAVING", "비용절감 Ao 영역", decision, marginal, knee),
        _step6_band_panel("TARGET", "계약목표 Ao 영역", decision, marginal, knee),
        _step6_band_panel("STABILITY_ENHANCED", "안정성강화 Ao 영역", decision, marginal, knee),
    ])

    # Cross-band shortage relations.
    pair_html = ""
    if (
        isinstance(decision, pd.DataFrame) and not decision.empty
        and {"Ao_Band_Name","Strategy","Stock_Units","NSGA_Cost_KRW","Probability_Any_Shortage"}.issubset(decision.columns)
    ):
        lab = decision["Ao_Band_Name"].astype(str) + " / " + decision["Strategy"].astype(str)
        y = pd.to_numeric(decision["Probability_Any_Shortage"], errors="coerce") * 100.0
        stock_svg = _dash_scatter(
            pd.to_numeric(decision["Stock_Units"], errors="coerce"),
            y, lab,
            xtitle="총 재고 (EA)", ytitle="미래 재고부족 발생확률 (%)",
            groups=decision.get("Ao_Band_ID", pd.Series("TARGET", index=decision.index)),
            point_ids=decision.get("Alternative_ID", pd.Series("", index=decision.index)),
            point_radius=5.8,
        )
        cost_svg = _dash_scatter(
            pd.to_numeric(decision["NSGA_Cost_KRW"], errors="coerce") / 1e8,
            y, lab,
            xtitle="총 비용 (억원)", ytitle="미래 재고부족 발생확률 (%)",
            groups=decision.get("Ao_Band_ID", pd.Series("TARGET", index=decision.index)),
            point_ids=decision.get("Alternative_ID", pd.Series("", index=decision.index)),
            point_radius=5.8,
        )
        pair_html = (
            '<div class="two-col">'
            '<div class="chart-card"><div class="chart-title">재고수량 ↔ 미래 재고부족 위험</div>'
            f'{stock_svg}</div>'
            '<div class="chart-card"><div class="chart-title">비용 ↔ 미래 재고부족 위험</div>'
            f'{cost_svg}</div></div>'
        )

    # Future shortage probability.
    future_svg = ""
    fut = future.copy() if isinstance(future, pd.DataFrame) else pd.DataFrame()
    if not fut.empty:
        if "Period_Months" in fut.columns:
            fut = fut[
                pd.to_numeric(fut["Period_Months"], errors="coerce")
                == int(decision_cfg.decision_period_months)
            ]
        if (
            "Evaluation_Method" in fut.columns
            and "건수 가중" in fut["Evaluation_Method"].astype(str).values
        ):
            fut = fut[fut["Evaluation_Method"].astype(str) == "건수 가중"]
        if {"Ao_Band_Name","Strategy","Probability_Zero_Shortage"}.issubset(fut.columns):
            lab = fut["Ao_Band_Name"].astype(str) + " / " + fut["Strategy"].astype(str)
            risk = (1 - pd.to_numeric(fut["Probability_Zero_Shortage"], errors="coerce").fillna(0)) * 100.0
            future_svg = _dash_bar(lab.tolist(), risk.tolist(), "미래 재고부족 발생확률 (%)")

    # Cost-Ao-risk bubble.
    bubble_svg = ""
    if (
        isinstance(reps, pd.DataFrame) and not reps.empty
        and isinstance(decision, pd.DataFrame) and not decision.empty
    ):
        _bubble_cols = [
            c for c in [
                "Alternative_ID",
                "Ao_Band_ID",
                "Ao_Band_Name",
                "Strategy",
                "NSGA_Cost_KRW",
                "Nominal_Ao",
            ]
            if c in reps.columns
        ]
        q = reps[_bubble_cols].merge(
            decision[["Alternative_ID","Probability_Any_Shortage"]].drop_duplicates("Alternative_ID"),
            on="Alternative_ID", how="inner", validate="one_to_one"
        )
        if not q.empty:
            lab = q["Ao_Band_Name"].astype(str) + " / " + q["Strategy"].astype(str)
            bubble_svg = _dash_cost_ao_risk_bubble(
                pd.to_numeric(q["NSGA_Cost_KRW"], errors="coerce") / 1e8,
                pd.to_numeric(
                    q.get("Nominal_Ao", pd.Series(np.nan, index=q.index)),
                    errors="coerce",
                ) * 100.0,
                lab,
                pd.to_numeric(q["Probability_Any_Shortage"], errors="coerce") * 100.0,
                groups=q.get("Ao_Band_ID", pd.Series("TARGET", index=q.index)),
                point_ids=q.get("Alternative_ID", pd.Series("", index=q.index)),
            )

    # Dynamic stock-domain note.
    bound_note = ""
    cand = nsga_result.get("candidates_df", pd.DataFrame()).copy()
    if isinstance(cand, pd.DataFrame) and not cand.empty and "stock_search_upper_bound" in cand.columns:
        ub = pd.to_numeric(cand["stock_search_upper_bound"], errors="coerce").dropna()
        if not ub.empty:
            bound_note = (
                '<div class="decision-note"><b>수리부속 탐색범위</b><br>'
                '기존 3/4/6 절대상한과 999 hard clip은 사용하지 않습니다. '
                '품목별 확률기반 탐색범위를 적용하며, 현재 후보품목 탐색상한은 '
                f'최소 {int(ub.min())}EA / 중앙값 {int(ub.median())}EA / 최대 {int(ub.max())}EA입니다. '
                '이는 구매 권고량이 아니라 NSGA의 탐색범위입니다.</div>'
            )

    rep_table = ""
    if isinstance(reps, pd.DataFrame) and not reps.empty:
        cols = [c for c in [
            "Ao_Band_Name","Strategy","Alternative_ID","Stock_Units",
            "NSGA_Cost_KRW","Nominal_Ao","Worst_Ao","Tail_Risk"
        ] if c in reps.columns]
        rep_table = reps[cols].to_html(index=False, classes="data-table", border=0, na_rep="-")

    decision_table = ""
    if isinstance(decision, pd.DataFrame) and not decision.empty:
        cols = [c for c in [
            "Ao_Band_Name","Strategy","Alternative_ID","Probability_Any_Shortage",
            "Service_Mean","Service_P05","Shortage_P95",
            "Band_Additional_Cost_vs_MinStock_KRW",
            "Band_Risk_Reduction_vs_MinStock",
            "Band_Risk_Reduction_per_100M_KRW"
        ] if c in decision.columns]
        decision_table = decision[cols].to_html(index=False, classes="data-table", border=0, na_rep="-")

    audit7_table = audit7.to_html(index=False, classes="data-table", border=0, na_rep="-") if isinstance(audit7, pd.DataFrame) and not audit7.empty else ""
    audit_table = audit_all.to_html(index=False, classes="data-table", border=0, na_rep="-") if isinstance(audit_all, pd.DataFrame) and not audit_all.empty else ""

    astat = audit_result.get("audit_summary", {})
    audit_status = str(astat.get("overall_status", "-"))
    pass_count = int(astat.get("pass_count", 0))
    total_checks = int(astat.get("total_checks", 0))
    available_cells = int((matrix["Cell_Status"].astype(str) != "EMPTY").sum()) if isinstance(matrix, pd.DataFrame) and not matrix.empty else 0

    spare_detail_html = _build_spare_detail_html(reps, inventory)
    risk_grade_html = _build_risk_energy_grade_html(decision)
    risk_delta_svg = _dash_cost_delta_vs_risk_delta(decision)

    anchor_note = ""
    if isinstance(policy_anchor, pd.DataFrame) and not policy_anchor.empty:
        ar = policy_anchor.iloc[0]
        anchor_note = (
            '<div class="decision-note"><b>정책영역 기준점</b><br>'
            '계약목표 기준대안은 Target Ao를 만족하는 실제 대안 중 비용이 가장 낮은 대안입니다. '
            f'현재 기준대안 {_dash_esc(ar.get("Anchor_Alternative_ID",""))}: '
            f'비용 {float(ar.get("Anchor_Cost_KRW",0))/1e8:.2f}억원, '
            f'Ao {float(ar.get("Anchor_Ao",0))*100:.2f}%, '
            f'미래 부족위험 {float(ar.get("Anchor_Risk",0))*100:.2f}%. '
            '이보다 싸면서 Risk가 증가하면 비용절감·Risk수용, '
            '더 비싸면서 Risk가 감소하면 안정성강화·Risk저감으로 분류합니다.'
            '</div>'
        )

    interaction_script = """

<p class="muted">목표 운용가용도 수준을 먼저 정책적으로 설정하고, 같은 Ao 범위 안에서 비용·재고·미래 위험이 다른 전략들을 비교합니다.
